**Epic: #227 (Agnostic specialized components)**

## Goal
Stateful image crop tool with Idle/Dragging/Resizing states, crop region geometry, aspect ratio enforcement.

## Out of scope
Framework-specific adapter components (Leptos/Dioxus).

## Spec refs
`spec/components/specialized/image-cropper.md`

## Depends on
None

## Layer
Component

## Framework
None

## Test tier
Unit

## Fibonacci points
5

## Element/ref handling note

ImageCropper agnostic code should own crop state, transform math, constraints, keyboard intent, and ARIA/data attrs. Live image/canvas handles, natural-size loading, viewport measurement, pointer capture, and raster export belong in adapters via native handles and supplied metrics.

Stable string IDs remain valid for semantic ARIA relationships, hydration-stable markup, and `data-ars-*` hooks. They should not be used as a substitute for live element handles. Leptos adapters should prefer `NodeRef`/resolved DOM elements for live operations; Dioxus adapters should prefer `MountedData` or the Dioxus platform layer where available.

## Tests to add first
- State machine transitions.
- Crop region positioning (x, y, width, height).
- Aspect ratio lock.
- Drag to move crop region.
- Resize from handles.
- Zoom controls.
- Rotation.
- Min/max crop size.
- ConnectApi: image container with overlay. Crop handles with appropriate roles.
- Output: crop geometry data.
- `data-ars-state` attrs.

## Acceptance criteria
- All public types documented per workspace `missing_docs` lint.
- State machine matches spec §1 exactly (for stateful/complex components).
- ConnectApi produces correct ARIA attributes per spec §2.
- Snapshot tests cover the component's `connect()` / `Api` `AttrMap` output for every anatomy part and each output-affecting state, prop, or context branch, with committed `.snap` fixtures passing `cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject`.
- All named tests pass with zero warnings.

## Spec impact
No spec change required.

## Board update rule
If blocked, keep `Status` in `Todo`, use native GitHub issue dependencies, and name the dependency or spec ambiguity.

12	# ImageCropper
13	
14	An `ImageCropper` lets the user select a rectangular (or circular) region of an image
15	for cropping. It supports drag-to-move, handle-resize, aspect ratio constraints, and
16	zoom/rotation.
17	
18	The following types define the crop data model:
19	
20	```rust
21	/// The crop area in normalized coordinates [0.0, 1.0] relative to the image.
22	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
23	pub struct CropArea {
24	    /// Left edge as a fraction of image width.
25	    pub x: f64,
26	    /// Top edge as a fraction of image height.
27	    pub y: f64,
28	    /// Width as a fraction of image width.
29	    pub width: f64,
30	    /// Height as a fraction of image height.
31	    pub height: f64,
32	    /// Rotation in degrees.
33	    pub rotation: f64,
34	}
35	
36	impl Default for CropArea {
37	    fn default() -> Self {
38	        Self { x: 0.1, y: 0.1, width: 0.8, height: 0.8, rotation: 0.0 }
39	    }
40	}
41	
42	/// Which handle of the crop area the user is interacting with.
43	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
44	pub enum CropHandle {
45	    TopLeft,
46	    TopRight,
47	    BottomLeft,
48	    BottomRight,
49	    Top,
50	    Bottom,
51	    Left,
52	    Right,
53	}
54	
55	/// Aspect ratio constraint for the crop area.
56	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
57	pub enum AspectRatio {
58	    /// No constraint.
59	    Free,
60	    /// Fixed width:height ratio.
61	    Fixed(f64),
62	    /// 1:1 square.
63	    Square,
64	    /// 4:3 landscape.
65	    Landscape4x3,
66	    /// 3:4 portrait.
67	    Portrait3x4,
68	    /// 16:9 wide.
69	    Wide16x9,
70	}
71	
72	impl AspectRatio {
73	    /// Get the ratio as a float.
74	    pub fn as_ratio(&self) -> Option<f64> {
75	        match self {
76	            Self::Free => None,
77	            Self::Fixed(r) => Some(*r),
78	            Self::Square => Some(1.0),
79	            Self::Landscape4x3 => Some(4.0 / 3.0),
80	            Self::Portrait3x4 => Some(3.0 / 4.0),
81	            Self::Wide16x9 => Some(16.0 / 9.0),
82	        }
83	    }
84	}
85	
86	/// Output format for the cropped image.
87	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
88	pub enum CropOutputFormat {
89	    /// PNG format.
90	    Png,
91	    /// JPEG format.
92	    Jpeg { quality: u8 },
93	    /// WebP format.
94	    WebP { quality: u8 },
95	}
96	
97	/// Flip state for the image.
98	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
99	pub struct FlipState {
100	    /// Horizontal flip.
101	    pub horizontal: bool,
102	    /// Vertical flip.
103	    pub vertical: bool,
104	}
105	
106	/// Result of a crop operation.
107	///
108	/// All coordinates are normalized to [0.0, 1.0] relative to the original
109	/// image dimensions. This allows the crop to be applied server-side
110	/// regardless of the display size used during cropping.
111	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
112	pub struct CropResult {
113	    /// Left edge as a fraction of original image width.
114	    pub x: f64,
115	    /// Top edge as a fraction of original image height.
116	    pub y: f64,
117	    /// Width as a fraction of original image width.
118	    pub width: f64,
119	    /// Height as a fraction of original image height.
120	    pub height: f64,
121	    /// Rotation in degrees applied to the image before cropping.
122	    pub rotation: f64,
123	    /// Scale factor applied (1.0 = no zoom).
124	    pub scale: f64,
125	    /// Aspect ratio constraint if one was active (e.g., `Some(16.0 / 9.0)`).
126	    pub aspect_ratio: Option<f64>,
127	    /// Flip state applied to the image.
128	    pub flip: FlipState,
129	}
130	
131	impl CropResult {
132	    /// Build a `CropResult` from a `CropArea` and current zoom/aspect/flip state.
133	    pub fn from_crop_area(area: &CropArea, zoom: f64, aspect: &AspectRatio, flip: FlipState) -> Self {
134	        CropResult {
135	            x: area.x,
136	            y: area.y,
137	            width: area.width,
138	            height: area.height,
139	            rotation: area.rotation,
140	            scale: zoom,
141	            aspect_ratio: aspect.as_ratio(),
142	            flip,
143	        }
144	    }
145	}
146	```

154	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
155	pub enum State {

157	    Idle,

294	
295	### 1.5 Full Machine Implementation
296	
297	```rust
298	/// Resize the crop area based on handle position and pointer delta.
299	/// Enforces aspect ratio constraints and boundary clamping.
300	fn resize_crop_area(ctx: &mut Context, handle: CropHandle, x: f64, y: f64) {
301	    if let (Some((ox, oy)), Some(ref start)) = (ctx.drag_origin, &ctx.drag_start_crop) {
302	        let dx = x - ox;
303	        let dy = y - oy;
304	        let mut crop = *start;
305	
306	        match handle {
307	            CropHandle::TopLeft => {
308	                crop.x = (start.x + dx).clamp(0.0, start.x + start.width - 0.05);
309	                crop.y = (start.y + dy).clamp(0.0, start.y + start.height - 0.05);
310	                crop.width = start.width - (crop.x - start.x);
311	                crop.height = start.height - (crop.y - start.y);
312	            }
313	            CropHandle::TopRight => {
314	                crop.y = (start.y + dy).clamp(0.0, start.y + start.height - 0.05);
315	                crop.width = (start.width + dx).clamp(0.05, 1.0 - start.x);
316	                crop.height = start.height - (crop.y - start.y);
317	            }
318	            CropHandle::BottomLeft => {
319	                crop.x = (start.x + dx).clamp(0.0, start.x + start.width - 0.05);
320	                crop.width = start.width - (crop.x - start.x);
321	                crop.height = (start.height + dy).clamp(0.05, 1.0 - start.y);
322	            }
323	            CropHandle::BottomRight => {
324	                crop.width = (start.width + dx).clamp(0.05, 1.0 - start.x);
325	                crop.height = (start.height + dy).clamp(0.05, 1.0 - start.y);
326	            }
327	            CropHandle::Top => {
328	                crop.y = (start.y + dy).clamp(0.0, start.y + start.height - 0.05);
329	                crop.height = start.height - (crop.y - start.y);
330	            }
331	            CropHandle::Bottom => {
332	                crop.height = (start.height + dy).clamp(0.05, 1.0 - start.y);
333	            }
334	            CropHandle::Left => {
335	                crop.x = (start.x + dx).clamp(0.0, start.x + start.width - 0.05);
336	                crop.width = start.width - (crop.x - start.x);
337	            }
338	            CropHandle::Right => {
339	                crop.width = (start.width + dx).clamp(0.05, 1.0 - start.x);
340	            }
341	        }
342	
343	        // Enforce aspect ratio if set
344	        if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {
345	            crop.height = crop.width / ratio;
346	            // Re-clamp after aspect ratio enforcement
347	            if crop.y + crop.height > 1.0 {
348	                crop.height = 1.0 - crop.y;
349	                crop.width = crop.height * ratio;
350	            }
351	        }
352	
353	        ctx.crop.set(crop);
354	    }
355	}
356	
357	/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.
358	fn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {
359	    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {
360	        let mut crop = ctx.crop.get();
361	        crop.height = crop.width / ratio;
362	        if crop.y + crop.height > 1.0 {
363	            crop.height = 1.0 - crop.y;
364	            crop.width = crop.height * ratio;
365	        }
366	        ctx.crop.set(crop);
367	    }
368	}
369	
370	/// The machine for the `ImageCropper` component.
371	pub struct Machine;
372	
373	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
374	    type State = State;
375	    type Event = Event;
376	    type Context = Context;
377	    type Props = Props;
378	    type Messages = Messages;
379	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
380	
381	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
382	        let crop = match &props.crop {
383	            Some(c) => Bindable::controlled(*c),
384	            None => Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_crop),
385	        };
386	
387	        let ids = ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id);
388	        let locale = env.locale.clone();
389	        let messages = messages.clone();
390	
391	        (State::Idle, Context {
392	            crop,
393	            aspect_ratio: props.aspect_ratio,
394	            zoom: props.zoom,
395	            min_zoom: props.min_zoom,
396	            max_zoom: props.max_zoom,
397	            disabled: props.disabled,
398	            circular: props.circular,
399	            flip: props.flip,
400	            drag_origin: None,
401	            drag_start_crop: None,
402	            focused_part: None,
403	            locale,
404	            messages,
405	            ids,
406	        })
407	    }
408	
409	    fn transition(
410	        state: &Self::State,
411	        event: &Self::Event,
412	        ctx: &Self::Context,
413	        _props: &Self::Props,
414	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
415	        // Focus/Blur always pass through.
416	        match event {
417	            Event::Focus { part } => {
418	                let p = *part;
419	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
420	                    ctx.focused_part = Some(p);
421	                }));
422	            }
423	            Event::Blur { .. } => {
424	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| {
425	                    ctx.focused_part = None;
426	                }));
427	            }
428	            _ => {}
429	        }
430	
431	        if ctx.disabled { return None; }
432	
433	        match (state, event) {
434	            (State::Idle, Event::DragStart { x, y }) => {
435	                let x = *x; let y = *y;
436	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Dragging).apply(move |ctx| {
437	                    ctx.drag_origin = Some((x, y));
438	                    ctx.drag_start_crop = Some(ctx.crop.get());
439	                }))
440	            }
441	
442	            (State::Dragging, Event::DragMove { x, y }) => {
443	                let x = *x; let y = *y;
444	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
445	                    if let (Some((ox, oy)), Some(ref start)) =
446	                        (ctx.drag_origin, &ctx.drag_start_crop)
447	                    {
448	                        let dx = x - ox;
449	                        let dy = y - oy;
450	                        let mut new_crop = *start;
451	                        new_crop.x = (start.x + dx).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - start.width);
452	                        new_crop.y = (start.y + dy).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - start.height);
453	                        ctx.crop.set(new_crop);
454	                    }
455	                }).with_effect(PendingEffect::new("announce-crop-moved", |ctx, _props, _send| {
456	                    // NOTE: The adapter should debounce/throttle this announcement
457	                    // (e.g., at most once per 500ms) to avoid flooding the screen reader
458	                    // with rapid-fire announcements during continuous pointer movement.
459	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
460	                    platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.crop_moved)(&ctx.locale));
461	                    no_cleanup()
462	                })))
463	            }
464	
465	            (State::Dragging, Event::DragEnd) => {
466	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {
467	                    ctx.drag_origin = None;
468	                    ctx.drag_start_crop = None;
469	                }))
470	            }
471	
472	            (State::Idle, Event::ResizeStart { handle, x, y }) => {
473	                let handle = *handle; let x = *x; let y = *y;
474	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Resizing { handle }).apply(move |ctx| {
475	                    ctx.drag_origin = Some((x, y));
476	                    ctx.drag_start_crop = Some(ctx.crop.get());
477	                }))
478	            }
479	
480	            (State::Resizing { handle }, Event::ResizeMove { x, y }) => {
481	                let handle = *handle; let x = *x; let y = *y;
482	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
483	                    resize_crop_area(ctx, handle, x, y);
484	                }).with_effect(PendingEffect::new("announce-crop-resized", |ctx, _props, _send| {
485	                    // NOTE: The adapter should debounce/throttle this announcement
486	                    // (e.g., at most once per 500ms) to avoid flooding the screen reader
487	                    // with rapid-fire announcements during continuous pointer movement.
488	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
489	                    platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.crop_resized)(&ctx.locale));
490	                    no_cleanup()
491	                })))
492	            }
493	
494	            (State::Resizing { .. }, Event::ResizeEnd) => {
495	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {
496	                    ctx.drag_origin = None;
497	                    ctx.drag_start_crop = None;
498	                }))
499	            }
500	
501	            (_, Event::SetCropArea(area)) => {
502	                let area = *area;
503	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
504	                    ctx.crop.set(area);
505	                }))
506	            }
507	
508	            (_, Event::SetAspectRatio(ratio)) => {
509	                let ratio = *ratio;
510	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
511	                    ctx.aspect_ratio = ratio;
512	                    enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx);
513	                }))
514	            }
515	
516	            (_, Event::SetZoom(zoom)) => {
517	                let zoom = zoom.clamp(ctx.min_zoom, ctx.max_zoom);
518	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
519	                    ctx.zoom = zoom;
520	                }))
521	            }
522	
523	            (_, Event::SetRotation(rotation)) => {
524	                let rotation = *rotation;
525	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
526	                    let mut crop = ctx.crop.get();
527	                    crop.rotation = rotation;
528	                    ctx.crop.set(crop);
529	                }))
530	            }
531	
532	            (_, Event::FlipHorizontal) => {
533	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| {
534	                    ctx.flip.horizontal = !ctx.flip.horizontal;
535	                }))
536	            }
537	
538	            (_, Event::FlipVertical) => {
539	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| {
540	                    ctx.flip.vertical = !ctx.flip.vertical;
541	                }))
542	            }
543	
544	            (_, Event::Reset) => {
545	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {
546	                    ctx.crop.set(CropArea::default());
547	                    ctx.zoom = 1.0;
548	                    ctx.flip = FlipState::default();
549	                    ctx.drag_origin = None;
550	                    ctx.drag_start_crop = None;
551	                }))
552	            }
553	
554	            (_, Event::NudgeCrop { dx, dy }) => {
555	                let dx = *dx; let dy = *dy;
556	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
557	                    let mut crop = ctx.crop.get();
558	                    crop.x = (crop.x + dx).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - crop.width);
559	                    crop.y = (crop.y + dy).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - crop.height);
560	                    ctx.crop.set(crop);
561	                }))
562	            }
563	
564	            _ => None,
565	        }
566	    }
567	
568	    fn connect<'a>(
569	        state: &'a Self::State,
570	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
571	        props: &'a Self::Props,
572	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
573	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
574	        Api { state, ctx, props, send }
575	    }
576	}
577	```
578	
579	### 1.6 Connect / API
580	
581	```rust
582	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
583	#[scope = "image-cropper"]
584	pub enum Part {
585	    Root,
586	    Image,
587	    Overlay,
588	    CropArea,
589	    Grid,
590	    Handle { position: CropHandle },
591	    ZoomSlider,
592	    RotationSlider,
593	    ResetTrigger,
594	    Label,
595	}
596	
597	pub struct Api<'a> {
598	    state: &'a State,
599	    ctx: &'a Context,
600	    props: &'a Props,
601	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
602	}
603	
604	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
605	    pub fn is_dragging(&self) -> bool { matches!(self.state, State::Dragging) }
606	    pub fn is_resizing(&self) -> bool { matches!(self.state, State::Resizing { .. }) }
607	    pub fn crop(&self) -> CropArea { self.ctx.crop.get() }
608	    pub fn zoom(&self) -> f64 { self.ctx.zoom }
609	    pub fn flip(&self) -> FlipState { self.ctx.flip }
610	
611	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
612	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
613	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
614	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
615	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
616	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id());
617	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "application");
618	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.label)(&self.ctx.locale));
619	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::RoleDescription), (self.ctx.messages.role_description)(&self.ctx.locale));
620	        let state_str = match self.state {
621	            State::Idle => "idle",
622	            State::Dragging => "dragging",
623	            State::Resizing { .. } => "resizing",
624	        };
625	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), state_str);
626	        if self.ctx.disabled { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true); }
627	        if self.ctx.circular { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-circular"), true); }
628	        attrs
629	    }
630	
631	    pub fn image_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
632	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
633	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Image.data_attrs();
634	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
635	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
636	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("image"));
637	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Src, &self.props.src);
638	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), "true");
639	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-zoom"), format!("{}", self.ctx.zoom));
640	        let crop = self.ctx.crop.get();
641	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-rotation"),
642	            format!("{}deg", crop.rotation));
643	        let scale_x = if self.ctx.flip.horizontal { -1 } else { 1 };
644	        let scale_y = if self.ctx.flip.vertical { -1 } else { 1 };
645	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-flip-x"), scale_x.to_string());
646	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-flip-y"), scale_y.to_string());
647	        attrs
648	    }
649	
650	    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
651	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
652	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Overlay.data_attrs();
653	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
654	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
655	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("overlay"));
656	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), "true");
657	        attrs
658	    }
659	
660	    pub fn crop_area_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
661	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
662	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::CropArea.data_attrs();
663	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
664	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
665	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("crop-area"));
666	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, "0");
667	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Class, "ars-touch-none");
668	
669	        let crop = self.ctx.crop.get();
670	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-x"), format!("{:.4}", crop.x));
671	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-y"), format!("{:.4}", crop.y));
672	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-width"), format!("{:.4}", crop.width));
673	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-crop-height"), format!("{:.4}", crop.height));
674	
675	        if self.is_dragging() { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-dragging"), true); }
676	        if let Some(part) = self.ctx.focused_part {
677	            if part == "crop-area" {
678	                attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-focus-visible"), true);
679	            }
680	        }
681	        attrs
682	    }
683	
684	    pub fn grid_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
685	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
686	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Grid.data_attrs();
687	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
688	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
689	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), "true");
690	        attrs
691	    }
692	
693	    pub fn handle_attrs(&self, position: CropHandle) -> AttrMap {
694	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
695	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] =
696	            Part::Handle { position }.data_attrs();
697	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
698	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
699	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Class, "ars-touch-none");
700	
701	        let pos_str = match position {
702	            CropHandle::TopLeft => "top-left",
703	            CropHandle::TopRight => "top-right",
704	            CropHandle::BottomLeft => "bottom-left",
705	            CropHandle::BottomRight => "bottom-right",
706	            CropHandle::Top => "top",
707	            CropHandle::Bottom => "bottom",
708	            CropHandle::Left => "left",
709	            CropHandle::Right => "right",
710	        };
711	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-position"), pos_str);
712	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),
713	            (self.ctx.messages.handle_label)(pos_str, &self.ctx.locale));
714	
715	        if matches!(self.state, State::Resizing { handle } if handle == position) {
716	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-active"), true);
717	        }
718	        attrs
719	    }
720	
721	    pub fn zoom_slider_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
722	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
723	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ZoomSlider.data_attrs();
724	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
725	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
726	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "slider");
727	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow), format!("{:.2}", self.ctx.zoom));
728	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin), format!("{:.2}", self.ctx.min_zoom));
729	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax), format!("{:.2}", self.ctx.max_zoom));
730	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.zoom_slider_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
731	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, "0");
732	        attrs
733	    }
734	
735	    pub fn rotation_slider_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
736	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
737	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] =
738	            Part::RotationSlider.data_attrs();
739	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
740	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
741	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "slider");
742	        let crop = self.ctx.crop.get();
743	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow), format!("{:.0}", crop.rotation));
744	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin), "-180");
745	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax), "180");
746	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.rotation_slider_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
747	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, "0");
748	        attrs
749	    }
750	
751	    pub fn reset_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
752	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
753	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] =
754	            Part::ResetTrigger.data_attrs();
755	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
756	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
757	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button");
758	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),
759	            (self.ctx.messages.reset_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
760	        if self.ctx.disabled {
761	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Disabled, "true");
762	        }
763	        attrs
764	    }
765	
766	    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
767	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
768	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();
769	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
770	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
771	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("label"));
772	        attrs
773	    }
774	
775	    pub fn on_crop_area_keydown(&self, data: &KeyboardEventData, shift: bool) {
776	        let nudge = if shift { 0.1 } else { 0.01 };
777	        match data.key {
778	            KeyboardKey::ArrowRight => (self.send)(Event::NudgeCrop { dx: nudge, dy: 0.0 }),
779	            KeyboardKey::ArrowLeft => (self.send)(Event::NudgeCrop { dx: -nudge, dy: 0.0 }),
780	            KeyboardKey::ArrowDown => (self.send)(Event::NudgeCrop { dx: 0.0, dy: nudge }),
781	            KeyboardKey::ArrowUp => (self.send)(Event::NudgeCrop { dx: 0.0, dy: -nudge }),
782	            _ => {}
783	        }
784	    }
785	
786	    pub fn on_root_keydown(&self, data: &KeyboardEventData) {
787	        match data.key {
788	            KeyboardKey::Character('+') | KeyboardKey::Character('=') => {
789	                (self.send)(Event::SetZoom(self.ctx.zoom + 0.1));
790	            }
791	            KeyboardKey::Character('-') => {
792	                (self.send)(Event::SetZoom(self.ctx.zoom - 0.1));
793	            }
794	            KeyboardKey::Character('r') => {
795	                let crop = self.ctx.crop.get();
796	                (self.send)(Event::SetRotation(crop.rotation + 90.0));
797	            }
798	            KeyboardKey::Character('R') => {
799	                let crop = self.ctx.crop.get();
800	                (self.send)(Event::SetRotation(crop.rotation - 90.0));
801	            }
802	            KeyboardKey::Character('h') => {
803	                (self.send)(Event::FlipHorizontal);
804	            }
805	            KeyboardKey::Character('v') => {
806	                (self.send)(Event::FlipVertical);
807	            }
808	            _ => {}
809	        }
810	    }
811	
812	    pub fn on_crop_area_pointer_down(&self, x: f64, y: f64) {
813	        (self.send)(Event::DragStart { x, y });
814	    }
815	
816	    pub fn on_handle_pointer_down(&self, handle: CropHandle, x: f64, y: f64) {
817	        (self.send)(Event::ResizeStart { handle, x, y });
818	    }
819	
820	    pub fn on_reset(&self) {
821	        (self.send)(Event::Reset);
822	    }
823	}
824	
825	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
826	    type Part = Part;
827	
828	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
829	        match part {
830	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
831	            Part::Image => self.image_attrs(),
832	            Part::Overlay => self.overlay_attrs(),
833	            Part::CropArea => self.crop_area_attrs(),
834	            Part::Grid => self.grid_attrs(),
835	            Part::Handle { position } => self.handle_attrs(position),
836	            Part::ZoomSlider => self.zoom_slider_attrs(),
837	            Part::RotationSlider => self.rotation_slider_attrs(),
838	            Part::ResetTrigger => self.reset_trigger_attrs(),
839	            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
840	        }
841	    }
842	}
843	```
844	
845	## 2. Anatomy
846	
847	```text
848	ImageCropper
849	├── Root              (required -- role="application")
850	├── Image             (required -- the source image, possibly transformed)
851	├── Overlay           (required -- semi-transparent mask outside crop area)
852	├── CropArea          (required -- the selected region, focusable)
853	│   ├── Grid          (optional -- rule-of-thirds grid lines)
854	│   └── Handle x 8    (required -- resize handles on corners and edges)
855	├── ZoomSlider        (optional -- zoom control)
856	├── RotationSlider    (optional -- rotation control)
857	├── ResetTrigger      (optional -- reset to default crop)
858	└── Label             (required -- describes the cropper)
859	```
860	
861	| Part           | Element    | Key Attributes                                                   |
862	| -------------- | ---------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
863	| Root           | `<div>`    | `role="application"`, `aria-roledescription="cropper"`           |
864	| Image          | `<img>`    | `aria-hidden="true"`, zoom/rotation CSS custom properties        |
865	| Overlay        | `<div>`    | `aria-hidden="true"`, semi-transparent mask                      |
866	| CropArea       | `<div>`    | `tabindex="0"`, `ars-touch-none`, position CSS custom properties |
867	| Grid           | `<div>`    | `aria-hidden="true"`, rule-of-thirds overlay                     |
868	| Handle         | `<div>`    | `aria-label="Resize {position}"`, `ars-touch-none`               |
869	| ZoomSlider     | `<input>`  | `role="slider"`, `aria-valuenow/min/max`                         |
870	| RotationSlider | `<input>`  | `role="slider"`, `aria-valuenow/min/max`                         |
871	| ResetTrigger   | `<button>` | `aria-label` from messages                                       |
872	| Label          | `<label>`  | Labels the cropper                                               |
873	
874	**`touch-action: none` requirement:** The CropArea element and all resize Handle elements
875	MUST include `class="ars-touch-none"` from the companion stylesheet. Without this,
876	touch-initiated dragging and resizing on mobile devices triggers browser scroll/pan
877	instead of producing pointer events.
878	
879	## 3. Accessibility
880	
881	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
882	
883	| Attribute              | Element              | Value                                         |
884	| ---------------------- | -------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
885	| `role="application"`   | Root                 | Custom interaction model                      |
886	| `aria-label`           | Root                 | From `messages.label` (default: "Crop image") |
887	| `aria-roledescription` | Root                 | `"cropper"` -- generic type descriptor        |
888	| `role="slider"`        | ZoomSlider           | Zoom control                                  |
889	| `role="slider"`        | RotationSlider       | Rotation control                              |
890	| `aria-label`           | Handle               | `"Resize {position}"` from messages           |
891	| `tabindex="0"`         | CropArea             | Keyboard focusable for arrow-key nudging      |
892	| `aria-hidden="true"`   | Image, Overlay, Grid | Decorative/structural elements                |
893	
894	### 3.2 Keyboard Interaction
895	
896	| Key         | Element  | Action                    |
897	| ----------- | -------- | ------------------------- |
898	| Arrow keys  | CropArea | Nudge crop area (1% step) |
899	| Shift+Arrow | CropArea | Large nudge (10% step)    |
900	| +/-         | Root     | Zoom in/out               |
901	| r/R         | Root     | Rotate +/-90 degrees      |
902	| h           | Root     | Flip horizontal           |
903	| v           | Root     | Flip vertical             |
904	
905	### 3.3 Screen Reader Announcements
906	
907	The cropper includes a visually-hidden live region (`aria-live="polite"`) that
908	announces crop area changes:
909	
910	- "Crop area moved" -- after arrow-key nudging
911	- "Crop area resized" -- after handle resize
912	
913	## 4. Internationalization
914	
915	### 4.1 Messages
916	
917	```rust
918	/// The messages for the `ImageCropper` component.
919	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
920	pub struct Messages {
921	    /// Accessible label for the cropper root.
922	    pub label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
923	    /// Role description for the cropper root (default: "cropper").
924	    pub role_description: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
925	    /// Label template for resize handles (receives position name).
926	    pub handle_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
927	    /// Accessible label for the zoom slider (default: "Zoom").
928	    pub zoom_slider_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
929	    /// Accessible label for the rotation slider (default: "Rotation").
930	    pub rotation_slider_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
931	    /// Label for the reset button.
932	    pub reset_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
933	    /// Announcement when crop area is moved.
934	    pub crop_moved: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
935	    /// Announcement when crop area is resized.
936	    pub crop_resized: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
937	}
938	
939	impl Default for Messages {
940	    fn default() -> Self {
941	        Self {
942	            label: MessageFn::static_str("Crop image"),
943	            role_description: MessageFn::static_str("cropper"),
944	            handle_label: MessageFn::new(|pos, _locale| format!("Resize {}", pos)),
945	            zoom_slider_label: MessageFn::static_str("Zoom"),
946	            rotation_slider_label: MessageFn::static_str("Rotation"),
947	            reset_label: MessageFn::static_str("Reset crop"),
948	            crop_moved: MessageFn::static_str("Crop area moved"),
949	            crop_resized: MessageFn::static_str("Crop area resized"),
950	        }
951	    }
952	}
953	
954	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
955	```
956	
957	| Key                                   | Default (en-US)       | Purpose                    |
958	| ------------------------------------- | --------------------- | -------------------------- |
959	| `image_cropper.label`                 | `"Crop image"`        | Root aria-label            |
960	| `image_cropper.role_description`      | `"cropper"`           | Root aria-roledescription  |
961	| `image_cropper.handle_label`          | `"Resize {position}"` | Handle labels              |
962	| `image_cropper.zoom_slider_label`     | `"Zoom"`              | Zoom slider aria-label     |
963	| `image_cropper.rotation_slider_label` | `"Rotation"`          | Rotation slider aria-label |
964	| `image_cropper.reset_label`           | `"Reset crop"`        | Reset button label         |
965	| `image_cropper.crop_moved`            | `"Crop area moved"`   | SR announcement            |
966	| `image_cropper.crop_resized`          | `"Crop area resized"` | SR announcement            |
967	
968	RTL: Handle positions map correctly (`Left` <-> `Right`). Arrow key nudging
969	reverses horizontal direction so that `ArrowRight` always moves toward the inline-end.
970	
971	## 5. Library Parity
972	
973	> Compared against: Ark UI (`ImageCropper`).
974	
975	### 5.1 Props
976	
977	| Feature                        | ars-ui                           | Ark UI                                           | Notes                                         |
978	| ------------------------------ | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------- |
979	| `crop` / `defaultCrop`         | `crop` / `default_crop`          | `initialCrop`                                    | Equivalent; ars-ui adds controlled mode       |
980	| `aspectRatio`                  | `aspect_ratio`                   | `aspectRatio`                                    | Equivalent                                    |
981	| `cropShape`                    | `circular`                       | `cropShape` (circle/rectangle)                   | Equivalent                                    |
982	| `zoom` / `minZoom` / `maxZoom` | `zoom` / `min_zoom` / `max_zoom` | `zoom` / `minZoom` / `maxZoom`                   | Equivalent                                    |
983	| `rotation`                     | via `SetRotation` event          | `rotation` / `defaultRotation`                   | Equivalent                                    |
984	| `flip`                         | `flip`                           | `flip` / `defaultFlip`                           | Equivalent                                    |
985	| `fixedCropArea`                | --                               | `fixedCropArea`                                  | Ark prevents crop area resizing; niche        |
986	| `nudgeStep`                    | 0.01 (hardcoded)                 | `nudgeStep` / `nudgeStepShift` / `nudgeStepCtrl` | Ark has configurable nudge steps              |
987	| `zoomStep`                     | 0.1 (hardcoded)                  | `zoomStep`                                       | Ark has configurable zoom step                |
988	| `zoomSensitivity`              | --                               | `zoomSensitivity`                                | Ark has pinch zoom sensitivity                |
989	| `minWidth` / `minHeight`       | 0.05 (5% min)                    | `minWidth` / `minHeight`                         | Ark has pixel-based min; ars-ui uses fraction |
990	| `maxWidth` / `maxHeight`       | --                               | `maxWidth` / `maxHeight`                         | Ark has max crop dimensions                   |
991	| `src`                          | `src`                            | --                                               | ars-ui has image source prop                  |
992	| `disabled`                     | `disabled`                       | --                                               | ars-ui has disabled state                     |
993	| `translations`                 | `messages`                       | `translations`                                   | Equivalent                                    |
994	
995	**Gaps:** None critical. `fixedCropArea` and granular nudge/zoom step props are minor configurability that adapters can override.
996	
997	### 5.2 Anatomy
998	
999	| Part           | ars-ui                 | Ark UI      | Notes                       |
1000	| -------------- | ---------------------- | ----------- | --------------------------- |
1001	| Root           | `Root`                 | `Root`      | Equivalent                  |
1002	| Image          | `Image`                | `Image`     | Equivalent                  |
1003	| Overlay        | `Overlay`              | --          | ars-ui has mask overlay     |
1004	| CropArea       | `CropArea`             | `Selection` | Equivalent (different name) |
1005	| Grid           | `Grid`                 | `Grid`      | Equivalent                  |
1006	| Handle         | `Handle` (8 positions) | `Handle`    | Equivalent                  |
1007	| ZoomSlider     | `ZoomSlider`           | --          | ars-ui has zoom control     |
1008	| RotationSlider | `RotationSlider`       | --          | ars-ui has rotation control |
1009	| ResetTrigger   | `ResetTrigger`         | --          | ars-ui has reset button     |
1010	| Label          | `Label`                | --          | ars-ui has label            |
1011	| Viewport       | --                     | `Viewport`  | Ark has viewport wrapper    |
1012	
1013	**Gaps:** None. `Viewport` is a layout wrapper handled by the adapter.
1014	
1015	### 5.3 Events
1016

## Implementation Patterns for Agnostic Stateful Components in ars-components

Based on my exploration of the SignaturePad component (merged in commit 70fa4858, "Implement SignaturePad agnostic core (#295)") and related stateful components like ColorPicker, ColorWheel, Timer, and FileUpload, here are the exact implementation patterns you should follow for ImageCropper:

### 1. Directory Layout and Module Organization

**File Structure:**
Each component lives in its own subdirectory under `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/`:
```
/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/
├── mod.rs                      (Category-level re-exports)
├── signature_pad/
│   └── mod.rs                  (All code in single file)
├── color_picker/
│   └── mod.rs
├── color_wheel/
│   └── mod.rs
├── timer/
│   └── mod.rs
├── file_upload/
│   ├── mod.rs
│   └── tests.rs                (Optional separate test file)
└── image_cropper/              (For your new component)
    └── mod.rs
```

**All code is in a single `mod.rs` per component** — not split into separate files. The `specialized/mod.rs` declares public module re-exports only.

### 2. SignaturePad Structure and Use Statements

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs`

**Imports (lines 22-29) — copy this block verbatim:**

use ars_core::{
    AriaAttr, AttrMap, Bindable, Callback, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart,
    ConnectApi, Env, HasId, HtmlAttr, Locale, MessageFn, PendingEffect, RasterError, RasterImage,
    RasterPoint, RasterSpec, SignatureRasterizer, TransitionPlan, no_cleanup,
};
```

**Order of Definition (how the module unfolds):**

1. **Module-level doc comment** (lines 1-20) — describes the component's role, what the core owns vs. what adapters own, what effects are emitted
2. **Import block** (lines 22-29) — `alloc::`, `core::`, then `ars_core::`, then framework-specific crates (e.g., `ars_interactions::`)
3. **Data model types** (lines 31-163) — `SignaturePoint`, `SignatureStroke`, `SignatureData`, and helper enums/types (`SignatureFormat`, `SignatureExport`)
4. **State enum** (lines 196-206)
5. **Event enum** (lines 209-260)

14. **#[cfg(test)] mod tests** (lines 1169+)

### 3. Key Structures: State, Event, Context, Props, Messages, Machine, Part, Api

#### State Enum (lines 196-206)
```rust

pub enum State {
    Idle,
    Drawing,
    Completed,
}
```
—Simple, `Copy` for efficiency, variants are the source of truth for UI state.

#### Event Enum (lines 209-260)
```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Event {
    DrawStart { x: f64, y: f64, pressure: f64 },
    DrawMove { x: f64, y: f64, pressure: f64 },
    DrawEnd,
    Undo,
    Clear,
    Focus,
    Blur,
    SyncData,  // Controlled-value sync from parent
    SyncProps, // Config prop changes
}
```
—Includes user-interaction events, lifecycle events (Focus/Blur), and prop-sync events (SyncData, SyncProps) for controlled/uncontrolled mode.

#### Context Struct (lines 263-297)
```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Context {
    /// The accumulated signature data.
    pub data: Bindable<SignatureData>,  // ← Controlled/uncontrolled value
    pub current_stroke: Option<SignatureStroke>,
    pub disabled: bool,
    pub readonly: bool,
    pub pen_color: String,
    pub pen_width: f64,
    pub min_distance: f64,
    pub focused: bool,
    pub locale: Locale,
    pub messages: Messages,

```
—The runtime state holder. State machine transitions mutate context in closures.

#### Props Struct (lines 300-352)
```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]
pub struct Props {
    pub id: String,  // ← Must derive HasId
    pub data: Option<SignatureData>,  // ← Controlled slot
    pub default_data: SignatureData,  // ← Uncontrolled default
    pub disabled: bool,
    pub readonly: bool,
    pub pen_color: String,
    pub pen_width: f64,
    pub min_distance: f64,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub on_data_change: Option<Callback<dyn Fn(SignatureData) + Send + Sync>>,
}
```
—Owned by the parent. Note:
- `id` must be stable (never changes after init)
- `data: Option<T>` for controlled mode; `default_data: T` for uncontrolled
- Callbacks are `Option<Callback<dyn Fn(...)>>` — trait object callbacks for flexibility

#### Messages Struct (lines 436-468)
```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Messages {
    pub canvas_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    pub clear_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    pub undo_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    pub signature_provided: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    pub signature_cleared: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    pub guide_text: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
}

impl Default for Messages { ... }
impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
```
—`MessageFn` wraps locale-aware message generators. Always derive `ComponentMessages`.

#### Effect Enum (lines 473-500)
```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum Effect {
    DrawingListeners,  // Adapter wires up pointer-drag listeners
    AnnounceProvided,  // Announce to aria-live region
    AnnounceCleared,   // Announce to aria-live region
    DataChange,        // Fire Props::on_data_change callback
}
```
—Named effect intents emitted by transitions; adapters dispatch on these names.

#### Machine Struct & Implementation (line 530 + lines 532-843)
```rust
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Machine;

impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
    type State = State;
    type Event = Event;
    type Context = Context;
    type Props = Props;
    type Messages = Messages;
    type Effect = Effect;
    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;

    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {
        let data = if let Some(data) = &props.data {
            Bindable::controlled(data.clone())
        } else {
            Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_data.clone())
        };
        // ...
    }

    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {
        // Return events to dispatch if props changed
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        if old.data != new.data {
            events.push(Event::SyncData);
        }
        // ...
        events
    }

    fn transition(
        state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        ctx: &Self::Context,
        props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
        // Return Some(plan) to transition, None to ignore
    }

    fn connect<'a>(...) -> Self::Api<'a> {
        Api { state, ctx, props, send }
    }
}
```

**Key patterns:**
- `init` sets up `Bindable::controlled()` or `Bindable::uncontrolled()` for controlled/uncontrolled mode
- `on_props_changed` returns a `Vec<Event>` of events to dispatch if props changed
- `transition` returns `Option<TransitionPlan<Self>>` — `Some(plan)` to transition, `None` to ignore the event
- `connect` returns the Api with all four pieces (`&state`, `&ctx`, `&props`, `send` closure)

### 4. Part Enum and ComponentPart Macro

**File:** signature_pad/mod.rs, lines 862-885:
```rust
#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "signature-pad"]
pub enum Part {
    /// Root wrapper element.
    Root,

    /// The `<canvas>` drawing surface.
    Canvas,

    /// Button that clears all strokes.
    ClearTrigger,

    /// Button that undoes the last stroke.
    UndoTrigger,

    /// Label describing the signature area.
    Label,

    /// Guide line or placeholder text (hidden once strokes exist).
    Guide,

    /// Hidden input carrying the SVG path data for form submission.
    HiddenInput,
}
```

**Usage in Api:**
```rust
pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
    // Part::Root.data_attrs() returns [(scope_attr_name, scope_value), (part_attr_name, part_value)]
    attrs
        .set(scope_attr, scope_val)  // Sets data-ars-scope="signature-pad"
        .set(part_attr, part_val)    // Sets data-ars-part="root"
        .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id());
    // ...
}
```

**What `#[derive(ComponentPart)]` + `#[scope = "..."]` does:**
- Generates a `.data_attrs()` method on each variant
- Returns a 2-tuple of `(attr_name, attr_value)` pairs
- `scope_attr` becomes `HtmlAttr::Data("ars-scope")`, `scope_val` is the kebab-cased component name (e.g., "signature-pad")
- `part_attr` becomes `HtmlAttr::Data("ars-part")`, `part_val` is the kebab-cased variant name (e.g., "root", "clear-trigger")

### 5. Api Struct and ConnectApi Trait

**File:** signature_pad/mod.rs, lines 888-1166:
```rust
pub struct Api<'a> {
    state: &'a State,
    ctx: &'a Context,
    props: &'a Props,
    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
}

impl Debug for Api<'_> { ... }

impl Api<'_> {
    // Computed state accessors
    pub const fn is_drawing(&self) -> bool { matches!(self.state, State::Drawing) }
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool { self.ctx.data.get().is_empty() }
    pub fn data(&self) -> &SignatureData { self.ctx.data.get() }

    // Attributes for each Part
    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn canvas_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn clear_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    // ... one per Part variant

    // Event dispatchers
    pub fn on_canvas_pointer_down(&self, x: f64, y: f64, pressure: f64) {
        (self.send)(Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure });
    }
}

impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
    type Part = Part;

    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
        match part {
            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
            Part::Canvas => self.canvas_attrs(),
            // ...
        }
    }
}
```

**Key patterns:**
- Each `*_attrs()` method calls `Part::*variant*.data_attrs()` to get scope/part data attributes
- Accessors read from the context: `self.ctx.data.get()`, `self.state`, etc.
- Event dispatchers invoke `(self.send)(Event::...)` to queue events
- Implement `ConnectApi` to unify all `part_attrs()` calls under a single `part_attrs(Part) -> AttrMap` dispatch

### 6. Bindable<T> for Controlled/Uncontrolled Values

**ColorPicker Example (file: color_picker/mod.rs, lines 268, 623, 650, 807-809, 1327):**

In Context:
```rust
pub struct Context {
    pub value: Bindable<ColorValue>,
    // ...
}
```

In Machine::init:
```rust
let value = if let Some(color) = props.value {
    Bindable::controlled(color)
} else {
    Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value)
};
```

In transition closures:
```rust
// Read the pending (internal) value during a drag
let base = *ctx.value.pending();  // Dereference the ref to clone

// Write back the mutated value
ctx.value.set(next);
```

In Event::SyncValue handler (controlled-value sync from parent):
```rust
(_, Event::SyncValue(value)) => {
    let value = *value;
    Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
        if let Some(color) = value {
            ctx.value.set(color);
        }
        ctx.value.sync_controlled(value);  // Update the controlled slot
    }))
}
```

In Api accessors:
```rust
pub const fn value(&self) -> &ColorValue {
    self.ctx.value.pending()  // Borrow, don't clone
}

pub fn value_as_string(&self) -> String {
    let color = self.ctx.value.pending();
    // Format it...
}
```

**Key Bindable methods:**
- `.pending()` → `&T` — the internal working copy (used during transitions)
- `.get()` → `&T` — the controlled or internal value (stable between prop syncs)
- `.set(value: T)` — mutate the internal value
- `.controlled(value: T)` → `Bindable` — mark as controlled
- `.uncontrolled(value: T)` → `Bindable` — mark as uncontrolled
- `.sync_controlled(value: Option<T>)` — sync controlled slot after parent update

### 7. Specialized Module Registration

**File:** `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs`
```rust

pub mod angle_slider;
pub mod clipboard;
pub mod color_area;
// ... one pub mod declaration per component directory

pub mod signature_pad;

```

**File:** `crates/ars-components/src/lib.rs`

```

The crate's `lib.rs` only declares `pub mod specialized;` — the specialized module itself does the per-component re-exports. No further re-exports are needed at the crate root for specialized components.

### 8. Summary: The Standard Pattern

Every stateful specialized component follows this order:

1. **Module doc** — what core owns, what adapters own, what effects are emitted
2. **Imports** — `alloc::`, `core::`, `ars_core::`, then framework crates
3. **Data types** — domain-specific (Point, Stroke, Data, Color, etc.)
4. **State enum** — `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]`
5. **Event enum** — includes SyncData/SyncProps for prop-driven updates
6. **Effect enum** — `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]`
7. **Context struct** — holds runtime state; uses `Bindable<T>` for controlled values
8. **Props struct** — derives `HasId`; includes `Option<T>` for controlled slot and callback slots
9. **Messages struct** — derives `ComponentMessages`; all messages are `MessageFn`
10. **Machine struct** + trait impl — `init`, `on_props_changed`, `transition`, `connect`, optional `initial_effects`
11. **Helper functions** — effect builders, transition helpers, channel getters, etc.
12. **Part enum** — derives `ComponentPart`, has `#[scope = "kebab-case"]`
13. **Api struct** — holds `(&state, &ctx, &props, &send)`; implements `ConnectApi`
14. **Tests** — snapshot tests and transition tests with `Service`

For **ImageCropper**, apply this template directly, substituting domain-specific types (crop region, constraints, resize handles, etc.) where signature_pad has stroke data.

## Summary

I've located and verified all the exact definitions, signatures, and file paths for the core engine types and helpers you need for your framework-agnostic stateful component implementation. Here's the complete reference:

---

### 1. **`ars_core::Machine` Trait**

**File:** `/crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs` lines 752-862

**Full trait definition:**

```rust
pub trait Machine: Sized + 'static {
    type State: Clone + Debug + PartialEq;
    type Event: Clone + Debug;
    type Context: Clone + Debug;
    type Props: Clone + PartialEq + HasId;
    type Messages: ComponentMessages + Clone + Default + 'static;
    type Effect: EffectName;
    type Api<'a>: ConnectApi where Self: 'a;

    fn init(
        props: &Self::Props,
        env: &Env,
        messages: &Self::Messages,
    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context);

    fn transition(
        state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        context: &Self::Context,
        props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>>;

    fn connect<'a>(
        state: &'a Self::State,
        context: &'a Self::Context,
        props: &'a Self::Props,
        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
    ) -> Self::Api<'a>;

    fn on_props_changed(_old: &Self::Props, _new: &Self::Props) -> Vec<Self::Event> {
        Vec::new()
    }

    fn initial_effects(

}

### 10. **`Locale` & `ComponentPart` Derive + `#[scope]`**

**Locale:** Re-exported from `ars_i18n` in `/crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs` line 63-66.

**`ComponentPart` derive macro:** `/crates/ars-derive/src/component_part.rs` lines 1-158

```rust
#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "component-name"]  // Required attribute
pub enum MyPart {
    Root,  // First variant becomes ROOT
    Other,
    WithField {
        #[part(default = "expr")]
        id: String,
    },
}

// Generated trait (ars-core):
pub trait ComponentPart: Clone + Debug + PartialEq + Eq + Hash + 'static {
    const ROOT: Self;
    fn scope() -> &'static str;
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str;
    fn all() -> Vec<Self>;
    fn data_attrs(&self) -> [(HtmlAttr, &'static str); 2] {
        [
            (HtmlAttr::Data("ars-scope"), Self::scope()),
            (HtmlAttr::Data("ars-part"), self.name()),
        ]
    }
}
```

`data_attrs()` returns a **2-element array** of `(HtmlAttr, &'static str)` tuples representing the scope and part name.

}

// Generated trait (ars-core):

    fn id(&self) -> &str;
    fn with_id(self, id: String) -> Self;
    fn set_id(&mut self, id: String);
}
```

**Requires:** A struct with a single public `id: String` field. No helper attributes.

## Test Patterns for Stateful Components in ars-components

Based on my exploration of SignaturePad (#295) and color_picker components (#297-299), here's the exact test structure you should follow for ImageCropper:

### 1. Unit Tests Location and Structure

**File path:** Inline in the component's `mod.rs`
- **Example:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs` (lines 1168-2399)

**Module structure:**
```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use alloc::{boxed::Box, vec, vec::Vec};
    use ars_core::{AttrMap, Env, Machine as _, NullSignatureRasterizer, RasterFormat, Service};
    use insta::assert_snapshot;
    use super::*;

    // ───────────────────────── helpers ─────────────────────────
    
    fn point(x: f64, y: f64) -> SignaturePoint { ... }
    fn stroke(points: &[(f64, f64)]) -> SignatureStroke { ... }
    fn data_with(n: usize) -> SignatureData { ... }
    fn test_props() -> Props { ... }
    fn fresh_service(props: Props) -> Service<Machine> { ... }
    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String { ... }
    fn api_for(...) -> Api<'static> { ... }
    
    // ───────────────────────── data model ─────────────────────────
    #[test]
    fn signature_data_default_is_empty() { ... }
    
    // ───────────────────────── props / init ─────────────────────────
    #[test]
    fn init_empty_data_starts_idle_uncontrolled() { ... }
    
    // ───────────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────────
    #[test]
    fn draw_start_enters_drawing_and_seeds_stroke() { ... }
    
    // ───────────────────────── snapshots ─────────────────────────
    #[test]
    fn snapshot_root_idle() {
        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).root_attrs()));
    }
}
```

**Organization sections in order:**
1. Helpers: test fixture builders
2. Data model: constructor, conversion, property tests
3. Props / init: initialization behavior
4. Transitions: state machine transitions
5. Prop sync: props change handling
6. Callbacks/Effects: side effects
7. Api accessors: connect() API surface
8. Snapshots: insta snapshot tests

---

### 2. Snapshot Tests with insta

**Location:** `src/specialized/{component}/snapshots/` directory
- **SignaturePad example:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/snapshots/`

**Snapshot file naming pattern:**
```
ars_components__specialized__{component}__tests__{test_name}.snap
```

**Example snapshot file:**
```
/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/snapshots/ars_components__specialized__signature_pad__tests__snapshot_root_idle.snap
```

**Content format** (from snapshot_root_idle.snap):
```
---
source: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs
expression: "snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).root_attrs())"
---
AttrMap {
    attrs: [
        (Data("ars-part"), String("root")),
        (Data("ars-scope"), String("signature-pad")),
        (Data("ars-state"), String("idle")),
        (Id, String("sig")),

```

**Test pattern:**
```rust
fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {
    format!("{attrs:#?}")
}

#[test]
fn snapshot_root_idle() {
    assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).root_attrs()));
}

#[test]
fn snapshot_canvas_interactive() {
    assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).canvas_attrs()));
}

// For color_picker (with explicit names):
#[test]
fn snapshot_root_closed() {
    let svc = service(Props { id: "cp".into(), ..Props::default() });
    assert_snapshot!(
        "color_picker_root_closed",
        snapshot_attrs(&svc.connect(&|_| {}).root_attrs())
    );
}
```

**Invocation for updates:**
```bash
cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject
```

This runs all snapshot tests, rejects unreferenced snapshots, and allows you to review/accept changes.

### 3. Proptest State-Machine Tests

**Location:** `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/`
- **Common config:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/common.rs`
- **Specialized tests:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs`

**Common helper** (read-only, use as-is):
```rust
// common.rs
pub(super) fn proptest_config() -> Config {
    let cases = std::env::var("PROPTEST_CASES")
        .ok()
        .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1000);

    Config {
        cases,
        failure_persistence: Some(Box::new(FileFailurePersistence::Direct(
            "proptest-regressions/state-machines.txt",
        ))),
        ..Config::default()
    }
}
```

**Per-component test pattern** (from specialized.rs):
```rust
mod color_area_proptests {
    use ars_components::specialized::color_area::{Event, Machine, Props};
    use ars_core::{AriaAttr, ColorChannel, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};
    use proptest::prelude::*;

    fn arb_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {
        prop_oneof![
            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragStart { x, y }),
            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragMove { x, y }),
            Just(Event::DragEnd),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::IncrementX { step }),
            any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),
            Just(Event::Blur),
        ]
    }

    proptest! {
        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]

        #[test]
        #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
        fn area_event_sequences_keep_value_valid(
            events in prop::collection::vec(arb_event(), 0..64),
        ) {
            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(
                Props { id: "ca".into(), ..Props::default() },
                &Env::default(),
                &Default::default(),
            );

            for ev in events {
                drop(svc.send(ev));
            }

            let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});

            // Assert invariants hold
            let value = *api.value();
            prop_assert!((0.0..360.0).contains(&value.hue));
            
            // connect() never panics
            let thumb = api.thumb_attrs();
            prop_assert!(thumb.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueText)).is_some());
        }
    }
}
```

**Key structure:**
1. Submodule per component: `mod {component}_proptests { ... }`
2. `arb_event()` function: returns `Strategy<Value = Event>`
3. `proptest! { #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())] ... }`
4. `#[ignore]` attribute (tests only run in nightly extended-proptest job)
5. Event vector generation: `prop::collection::vec(arb_event(), 0..64)`
6. Service creation and event feeding
7. `api = svc.connect()` to get the connect() output
8. Invariant assertions with `prop_assert!` macro

### 4. Spec-Conformance Tests

**Location:** `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/`
- **Helper:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/helper.rs`
- **Specialized tests:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs` (lines 294-315)

**Helper function** (from helper.rs):
```rust
pub(crate) fn assert_anatomy<P>(
    expected_scope: &'static str, 
    expected_parts: &[(P, &'static str)]
)
where
    P: ComponentPart + core::fmt::Debug + PartialEq,
{
    assert_eq!(P::scope(), expected_scope, "Part::scope() must match spec §3 anatomy");
    
    let actual = P::all();
    let actual_names = actual.iter().map(ComponentPart::name).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let expected_names = expected_parts.iter().map(|(_, name)| *name).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    
    assert_eq!(actual_names, expected_names, "Part::all() name list must equal spec §3 anatomy");
    
    // Round-trip checks for each part
    for (actual_part, (expected_part, expected_name)) in actual.iter().zip(expected_parts) {
        assert_eq!(actual_part.name(), *expected_name);
        assert_eq!(actual_part, expected_part);
    }
    
    if let Some((first_expected, _)) = expected_parts.first() {
        assert_eq!(&P::ROOT, first_expected, "Part::ROOT must equal first spec-declared part");
    }
}
```

**Test pattern** (SignaturePad example):
```rust
#[test]
fn signature_pad_anatomy_matches_spec() {
    assert_anatomy(
        "signature-pad",
        &[
            (specialized_core::signature_pad::Part::Root, "root"),
            (specialized_core::signature_pad::Part::Canvas, "canvas"),
            (specialized_core::signature_pad::Part::ClearTrigger, "clear-trigger"),
            (specialized_core::signature_pad::Part::UndoTrigger, "undo-trigger"),
            (specialized_core::signature_pad::Part::Label, "label"),
            (specialized_core::signature_pad::Part::Guide, "guide"),
            (specialized_core::signature_pad::Part::HiddenInput, "hidden-input"),
        ],
    );
}
```

**What it checks:**
1. `Part::scope()` matches `data-ars-scope` value from spec
2. `Part::all()` list is exactly the spec-declared parts in spec order
3. Every part round-trips through `Part::name()` with kebab-case value
4. `Part::ROOT` == first declared part (workspace convention)

### Summary for ImageCropper Implementation

**Your test checklist (TDD-first):**

1. **Inline unit tests** in `src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs`:
   - Organize by sections: helpers, data model, props/init, transitions, prop sync, callbacks, Api accessors, snapshots

2. **Snapshot tests** in same mod.rs:
   - Use `assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.part_attrs()))` for each part

4. **Spec-conformance** in `tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs`:

   - Pulled from spec's anatomy table §3

All patterns are identical to SignaturePad and color_picker — copy their structure directly.
