462	            undo_label: MessageFn::static_str("Undo last stroke"),
463	            signature_provided: MessageFn::static_str("Signature provided"),
464	            signature_cleared: MessageFn::static_str("Signature cleared"),
465	            guide_text: MessageFn::static_str("Sign here"),
466	        }
467	    }
468	}
469	
470	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
471	
472	/// Typed effect intents emitted by the signature-pad machine.
473	///
474	/// The agnostic core never touches the live canvas or the screen reader; it
475	/// emits these markers and the framework adapter performs the real work.
476	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
477	pub enum Effect {
478	    /// Adapter attaches global pointer-drag listeners (e.g. via
479	    /// `PlatformEffects::track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch [`Event::DrawMove`]
480	    /// while the pointer moves and [`Event::DrawEnd`] on pointer up, so a stroke
481	    /// continues even when the pointer leaves the canvas. Emitted on the
482	    /// transition into [`State::Drawing`].
483	    DrawingListeners,
484	
485	    /// Adapter announces [`Messages::signature_provided`] into a polite
486	    /// `aria-live` region. Emitted on the transition into [`State::Completed`]
487	    /// (pointer up after a valid stroke).
488	    AnnounceProvided,
489	
490	    /// Adapter announces [`Messages::signature_cleared`] into a polite
491	    /// `aria-live` region. Emitted on the [`Event::Clear`] transition.
492	    AnnounceCleared,
493	
494	    /// The signature data changed through user interaction (a committed stroke,
495	    /// undo, or clear). Fires [`Props::on_data_change`] with the new data so a
496	    /// parent holding controlled [`Props::data`] can update it — without this,
497	    /// controlled mode would never observe the change. Not emitted for
498	    /// [`Event::SyncData`] (that change originates from the parent).
499	    DataChange,
500	}
501	
502	/// The machine for the `SignaturePad` component.
503	///
504	/// # Examples
505	///
506	/// Draw a single stroke and complete it. In a real app the adapter dispatches
507	/// [`Event::DrawMove`]/[`Event::DrawEnd`] from the pointer-drag listeners that
508	/// [`Effect::DrawingListeners`] sets up; here we send them directly:
509	///
510	/// ```
511	/// use ars_components::specialized::signature_pad::{Event, Machine, Messages, Props, State};
512	/// use ars_core::{Env, Service};
513	///
514	/// let mut pad = Service::<Machine>::new(
515	///     Props::new().id("sig").min_distance(0.0),
516	///     &Env::default(),
517	///     &Messages::default(),
518	/// );
519	/// assert_eq!(pad.state(), &State::Idle);
520	///
521	/// drop(pad.send(Event::DrawStart { x: 0.0, y: 0.0, pressure: 0.5 }));
522	/// assert_eq!(pad.state(), &State::Drawing);
523	///
524	/// drop(pad.send(Event::DrawMove { x: 10.0, y: 10.0, pressure: 0.5 }));
525	/// drop(pad.send(Event::DrawEnd));
526	/// assert_eq!(pad.state(), &State::Completed);
527	/// assert_eq!(pad.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);
528	/// ```
529	#[derive(Debug)]
530	pub struct Machine;
531	
532	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
533	    type State = State;
534	    type Event = Event;
535	    type Context = Context;
536	    type Props = Props;
537	    type Messages = Messages;
538	    type Effect = Effect;
539	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
540	
541	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {
542	        let data = if let Some(data) = &props.data {
543	            Bindable::controlled(data.clone())
544	        } else {
545	            Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_data.clone())
546	        };
547	
548	        let state = if data.get().is_empty() {
549	            State::Idle
550	        } else {
551	            State::Completed
552	        };
553	
554	        (
555	            state,
556	            Context {
557	                data,
558	                current_stroke: None,
559	                disabled: props.disabled,
560	                readonly: props.readonly,
561	                pen_color: props.pen_color.clone(),
562	                pen_width: props.pen_width,
563	                min_distance: props.min_distance,
564	                focused: false,
565	                locale: env.locale.clone(),
566	                messages: messages.clone(),
567	                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
568	            },
569	        )
570	    }
571	
572	    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {
573	        assert_eq!(
574	            old.id, new.id,
575	            "signature_pad::Props.id must remain stable after init"
576	        );
577	
578	        let mut events = Vec::new();
579	
580	        if old.data != new.data {
581	            events.push(Event::SyncData);
582	        }
583	
584	        if old.disabled != new.disabled
585	            || old.readonly != new.readonly
586	            || old.pen_color != new.pen_color
587	            || old.pen_width != new.pen_width
588	            || old.min_distance != new.min_distance
589	        {
590	            events.push(Event::SyncProps);
591	        }
592	
593	        events
594	    }
595	
596	    fn transition(
597	        state: &Self::State,
598	        event: &Self::Event,
599	        ctx: &Self::Context,
600	        props: &Self::Props,
601	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
602	        // Focus/Blur and the prop-sync events always pass through, regardless of
603	        // disabled/read-only (prop sync must be able to re-enable the pad).
604	        match event {
605	            Event::Focus => {
606	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {
607	                    ctx.focused = true;
608	                }));
609	            }
610	
611	            Event::Blur => {
612	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {
613	                    ctx.focused = false;
614	                }));
615	            }
616	
617	            Event::SyncProps => {
618	                let disabled = props.disabled;
619	                let readonly = props.readonly;
620	                let pen_color = props.pen_color.clone();
621	                let pen_width = props.pen_width;
622	                let min_distance = props.min_distance;
623	
624	                // Becoming non-interactive mid-stroke must not strand the machine
625	                // in `Drawing` — the disabled/read-only gate below would then
626	                // reject the trailing `DrawEnd`/`Clear`, leaving an in-flight
627	                // stroke that could later commit stale. Cancel it and leave
628	                // `Drawing` (blank canvas -> `Idle`, otherwise `Completed`).
629	                let cancel_drawing = (disabled || readonly) && matches!(state, State::Drawing);
630	                let target = if cancel_drawing {
631	                    if ctx.data.pending().is_empty() {
632	                        State::Idle
633	                    } else {
634	                        State::Completed
635	                    }
636	                } else {
637	                    *state
638	                };
639	
640	                return Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
641	                    ctx.disabled = disabled;
642	                    ctx.readonly = readonly;
643	                    ctx.pen_color = pen_color;
644	                    ctx.pen_width = pen_width;
645	                    ctx.min_distance = min_distance;
646	                    if cancel_drawing {
647	                        ctx.current_stroke = None;
648	                    }
649	                }));
650	            }
651	
652	            Event::SyncData => {
653	                let new_data = props.data.clone();
654	                // Emptiness after the sync: the new controlled value, or — when
655	                // dropping to uncontrolled — the retained local value.
656	
657	                let empty_after = if let Some(data) = &new_data {
658	                    data.is_empty()
659	                } else {
660	                    ctx.data.pending().is_empty()
661	                };
662	
663	                // Reconcile the displayed state with the synced data, but never
664	                // reorder an in-flight stroke.
665	                let plan = if matches!(state, State::Drawing) {
666	                    TransitionPlan::new()
667	                } else {
668	                    TransitionPlan::to(if empty_after {
669	                        State::Idle
670	                    } else {
671	                        State::Completed
672	                    })
673	                };
674	
675	                return Some(plan.apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
676	                    if let Some(data) = new_data {
677	                        ctx.data.set(data.clone());
678	                        ctx.data.sync_controlled(Some(data));
679	                    } else {
680	                        ctx.data.sync_controlled(None);
681	                    }
682	                }));
683	            }
684	
685	            _ => {}
686	        }
687	
688	        if ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly {
689	            return None;
690	        }
691	
692	        match (state, event) {
693	            // Start drawing: seed a new stroke and ask the adapter to wire up
694	            // the global pointer-drag listeners that feed DrawMove/DrawEnd.
695	            (State::Idle | State::Completed, Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure }) => {
696	                let point = SignaturePoint {
697	                    x: *x,
698	                    y: *y,
699	                    pressure: *pressure,
700	                    timestamp: 0.0,
701	                };
702	
703	                Some(
704	                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Drawing)
705	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
706	                            ctx.current_stroke = Some(SignatureStroke {
707	                                points: vec![point],
708	                            });
709	                        })
710	                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::DrawingListeners)),
711	                )
712	            }
713	
714	            // Continue drawing: append the point only when it is at least
715	            // `min_distance` away from the previous one (cull jitter).
716	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure }) => {
717	                let point = SignaturePoint {
718	                    x: *x,
719	                    y: *y,
720	                    pressure: *pressure,
721	                    timestamp: 0.0,
722	                };
723	
724	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
725	                    let min_distance = ctx.min_distance;
726	
727	                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.as_mut()
728	                        && let Some(last) = stroke.points.last()
729	                    {
730	                        let dx = point.x - last.x;
731	                        let dy = point.y - last.y;
732	
733	                        if (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt() >= min_distance {
734	                            stroke.points.push(point);
735	                        }
736	                    }
737	                }))
738	            }
739	
740	            // End drawing: commit the in-flight stroke if it has enough points.
741	            // A stroke that is too short to commit (a stray tap) leaves the
742	            // signature unchanged, so the pad returns to `Idle` when the canvas
743	            // is still blank and only announces when a stroke is actually added.
744	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawEnd) => {
745	                let commits = ctx
746	                    .current_stroke
747	                    .as_ref()
748	                    .is_some_and(|stroke| stroke.points.len() >= 2);
749	
750	                // Build on the *pending* (internal) data, not `get()`: in
751	                // controlled mode `get()` returns the stale parent value until it
752	                // round-trips through `on_data_change`, so reading it would drop
753	                // earlier un-synced strokes when several are drawn in quick
754	                // succession.
755	                let target = if commits || !ctx.data.pending().is_empty() {
756	                    State::Completed
757	                } else {
758	                    State::Idle
759	                };
760	
761	                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
762	                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take()
763	                        && stroke.points.len() >= 2
764	                    {
765	                        let mut data = ctx.data.pending().clone();
766	
767	                        data.strokes.push(stroke);
768	
769	                        ctx.data.set(data);
770	                    }
771	                });
772	
773	                if commits {
774	                    plan = plan
775	                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided))
776	                        .with_effect(data_change_effect());
777	                }
778	
779	                Some(plan)
780	            }
781	
782	            // Undo the last completed stroke. The post-pop emptiness — not the
783	            // stroke count — decides the target, so data carrying empty-stroke
784	            // entries can never strand the pad in `Completed` while it is blank.
785	            // Built on the pending working copy to preserve un-synced controlled
786	            // edits.
787	            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {
788	                let mut data = ctx.data.pending().clone();
789	                data.strokes.pop();
790	
791	                let target = if data.is_empty() {
792	                    State::Idle
793	                } else {
794	                    State::Completed
795	                };
796	
797	                Some(
798	                    TransitionPlan::to(target)
799	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
800	                            ctx.data.set(data);
801	                        })
802	                        .with_effect(data_change_effect()),
803	                )
804	            }
805	
806	            // Clear all strokes from any state.
807	            (_, Event::Clear) => {
808	                // Notify the parent only when there was something to clear, so a
809	                // clear on an already-blank pad does not emit a spurious change.
810	                let had_data = !ctx.data.pending().is_empty();
811	
812	                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)
813	                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
814	                        ctx.data.set(SignatureData::default());
815	                        ctx.current_stroke = None;
816	                    })
817	                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCleared));
818	
819	                if had_data {
820	                    plan = plan.with_effect(data_change_effect());
821	                }
822	
823	                Some(plan)
824	            }
825	
826	            _ => None,
827	        }
828	    }
829	
830	    fn connect<'a>(
831	        state: &'a Self::State,
832	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
833	        props: &'a Self::Props,
834	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
835	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
836	        Api {
837	            state,
838	            ctx,
839	            props,
840	            send,
841	        }
842	    }
843	}
844	
845	/// Builds the [`Effect::DataChange`] effect that notifies
846	/// [`Props::on_data_change`] with the new signature data.
847	///
848	/// Reads the bound value's *pending* (internal) data, which is the value just
849	/// committed by the transition — in controlled mode `get()` would still return
850	/// the stale parent-owned value until it round-trips back through props.
851	fn data_change_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
852	    PendingEffect::new(Effect::DataChange, |ctx: &Context, props: &Props, _send| {
853	        if let Some(callback) = &props.on_data_change {
854	            callback(ctx.data.pending().clone());
855	        }
856	
857	        no_cleanup()
858	    })
859	}
860	
861	/// DOM parts of the `SignaturePad` component.
862	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
863	#[scope = "signature-pad"]
864	pub enum Part {
865	    /// Root wrapper element.
866	    Root,
867	
868	    /// The `<canvas>` drawing surface.
869	    Canvas,
870	
871	    /// Button that clears all strokes.
872	    ClearTrigger,
873	
874	    /// Button that undoes the last stroke.
875	    UndoTrigger,
876	
877	    /// Label describing the signature area.
878	    Label,
879

205	/// Run the snapshot count lint.
206	///
207	/// # Errors
208	///
209	/// Returns [`Error::Failed`] when detected stateful components have fewer than
210	/// the required snapshot count.
211	pub fn check_snapshot_count(options: &SnapshotCountOptions) -> Result<String, Error> {
212	    let snapshots = collect_files(&options.snapshots_dir, |path| {
213	        path.extension()
214	            .is_some_and(|extension| extension == "snap")
215	    })?;
216	
217	    let total_snapshots = snapshots.len();
218	
219	    let workspace_root = workspace_root_for(&options.snapshots_dir);
220	
221	    let state_variants = detect_state_variants(&workspace_root)?;
222	
223	    let part_counts = detect_part_counts(&workspace_root)?;
224	
225	    let mut counts = BTreeMap::new();
226	
227	    for snapshot in snapshots {
228	        if let Some(component) = infer_snapshot_component(&snapshot)
229	            && state_variants.contains_key(&component)
230	        {
231	            *counts.entry(component).or_insert(0) += 1;
232	        }
233	    }
234	
235	    let mut output =
236	        String::from("Component | Snapshots | Variants | Parts | Min | Max | Status\n");
237	
238	    output.push_str("----------|-----------|----------|-------|-----|-----|-------\n");
239	
240	    let mut components = BTreeSet::new();
241	
242	    components.extend(counts.keys().cloned());
243	    components.extend(
244	        state_variants
245	            .iter()
246	            .filter_map(|(component, variants)| (*variants > 2).then_some(component.clone())),
247	    );
248	
249	    let mut failures = Vec::new();
250	    let mut warnings = Vec::new();
251	
252	    if total_snapshots > 500 {
253	        warnings.push(format!(
254	            "total snapshots={total_snapshots}, warning threshold=500"
255	        ));
256	    }
257	
258	    for component in components {
259	        let count = counts.get(&component).copied().unwrap_or(0);
260	
261	        let variants = state_variants.get(&component).copied().unwrap_or(0);
262	
263	        let parts = part_counts.get(&component).copied().unwrap_or(0);
264	
265	        let min_per_variant =
266	            snapshot_min_per_variant(&workspace_root, &component, options.min_per_variant);
267	
268	        let required = if variants > 2 {
269	            variants * min_per_variant
270	        } else {
271	            0
272	        };
273	
274	        // Soft per-component budget — derived from anatomy size when
275	        // detectable, falling back to the hard ceiling. The formula
276	        // matches `spec/testing/13-policies.md` §3.1:
277	        //
278	        //     budget = min(per_part_per_variant × variants × parts,
279	        //                  max_per_component)
280	        //
281	        // When a component's anatomy parts cannot be detected (no
282	        // `#[derive(ComponentPart)]` enum found, or the component
283	        // is not yet implemented), the formula collapses to
284	        // `max_per_component` so the hard ceiling still applies.
285	        let formula_budget = if variants > 0 && parts > 0 {
286	            options
287	                .per_part_per_variant
288	                .saturating_mul(variants)
289	                .saturating_mul(parts)
290	        } else {
291	            options.max_per_component
292	        };
293	
294	        let budget = formula_budget.min(options.max_per_component);
295	
296	        let mut status = "OK";
297	
298	        if required > 0 && count < required {
299	            failures.push(format!(
300	                "{component}: snapshots={count}, state_variants={variants}, required={required}"
301	            ));
302	
303	            status = "FAIL";
304	        }
305	
306	        if count > budget {
307	            failures.push(format!(
308	                "{component}: snapshots={count}, budget={budget} \
309	                 (formula = min({per_part} × {variants} × {parts}, {ceiling}))",
310	                per_part = options.per_part_per_variant,
311	                ceiling = options.max_per_component
312	            ));
313	
314	            status = "FAIL";
```

> AGENT

I now have everything needed. The spec's §1.5 is written in an outdated style that won't compile against the current engine, and it's missing the controlled-mode sync machinery that every recent stateful component (SignaturePad #295, ColorPicker/ColorArea #297-299) includes. Let me confirm the key design decision before finalizing the plan.

The mandatory engine-level reconciliations (non-negotiable, won't compile otherwise):
- Replace string-named `PendingEffect::new("announce-crop-moved", ...)` + inline `use_platform_effects()` calls with a typed `Effect` enum (`AnnounceCropMoved`, `AnnounceCropResized`) dispatched by the adapter — the agnostic core cannot call `use_platform_effects()`.
- `Bindable::get()` returns `&T`, so `let mut crop = ctx.crop.get()` becomes `let mut crop = *ctx.crop.get()` (CropArea is `Copy`).

The one genuine design decision is controlled-mode support:

## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Task #296 — Implement ImageCropper agnostic core

## Context

GitHub issue #296 (Epic #227, "Agnostic specialized components") asks for the
framework-agnostic core of `ImageCropper`: a stateful image crop tool with
`Idle`/`Dragging`/`Resizing` states, crop-region geometry, aspect-ratio
enforcement, zoom/rotation/flip, keyboard nudging, and ARIA/data attributes.
Adapters (Leptos/Dioxus), live canvas handles, and raster export are explicitly
**out of scope** — this is agnostic core only (Test tier: Unit, 5 points).

The contract is `spec/components/specialized/image-cropper.md`. During exploration
two kinds of spec drift surfaced that must be reconciled **in this PR** (per
CLAUDE.md "Spec Synchronization During Implementation"):

1. **Engine drift (mandatory — spec §1.5 won't compile as written).** The spec
   emits announcements with string-named `PendingEffect::new("announce-crop-moved", |ctx, _props, _send| { use_platform_effects().announce(...) })`.
   The current `ars_core::Machine` trait requires `type Effect: EffectName` and
   the agnostic core has **no** `use_platform_effects()` — that hook is
   adapter-only. The established convention (SignaturePad #295, ColorPicker
   #297-299) is a typed `Effect` enum whose variants the adapter dispatches.
   Also `Bindable::get()` returns `&T`, so `let mut crop = ctx.crop.get()` must
   become `let mut crop = *ctx.crop.get()` (`CropArea: Copy`).

2. **Controlled-mode drift (confirmed with user: add full sync).** The spec
   defines a controlled `crop: Option<CropArea>` prop and uses
   `Bindable::controlled`, but defines no change callback, no sync events, and
   no `on_props_changed`. Without them controlled mode is inert. We add the
   SignaturePad-style sync machinery and update the spec to match.

Intended outcome: a fully documented, tested, spec-synchronized `ImageCropper`
agnostic machine that passes `cargo xci-fast` and the post-implementation-audit.

## Approach

Single new module `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs`,
following the SignaturePad template verbatim
(`crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs`) for structure,
imports, and conventions. TDD: tests first.

### File layout & wiring
- New: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs`
- New: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/snapshots/` (insta)
- Edit: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs` — add `pub mod image_cropper;`
  (insert alphabetically).

### Module contents (mirror spec §1, adapted to current engine)
Order, per the SignaturePad template: module doc → imports → data types →
`State` → `Event` → `Effect` → `Context` → `Props` (`#[derive(HasId)]`) →
`Messages` (`#[derive(...)]` + `impl ComponentMessages`) → `Machine` + trait impl
→ helper fns → `Part` (`#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "image-cropper"]`) →
`Api` + `impl ConnectApi` → `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`.

Import block (from `ars_core`): `AriaAttr, AttrMap, Bindable, Callback,
ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, CssProperty, Env,
HasId, HtmlAttr, Locale, MessageFn, PendingEffect, TransitionPlan, no_cleanup`;
keyboard types (`KeyboardEventData`, `KeyboardKey`) from `ars_core` re-export or
`ars_interactions`.

**Data types** (verbatim from spec lines 20-146): `CropArea` (+ `Default`),
`CropHandle`, `AspectRatio` (+ `as_ratio`), `CropOutputFormat`, `FlipState`,
`CropResult` (+ `from_crop_area`). `CropArea` needs `impl BindableValue` (marker)
so it can sit in `Bindable<CropArea>`.

**`State`** (spec §1.1): `Idle`, `Dragging`, `Resizing { handle: CropHandle }`.

**`Event`** (spec §1.2) + **added sync events**: all spec events
(`DragStart/Move/End`, `ResizeStart/Move/End`, `SetCropArea`, `SetAspectRatio`,
`SetZoom`, `SetRotation`, `FlipHorizontal/Vertical`, `Reset`, `Focus`, `Blur`,
`NudgeCrop`) **plus** `SyncCrop` and `SyncProps` (new, for controlled mode).

**`Effect`** (new typed enum): `AnnounceCropMoved`, `AnnounceCropResized`,
`CropChange` (fires `on_crop_change`). Derives `Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq,
Eq, Hash` (satisfies the blanket `EffectName` impl).

**`Context`** (spec §1.3) — as specified (`crop: Bindable<CropArea>`,
`aspect_ratio`, `zoom`, `min_zoom`, `max_zoom`, `disabled`, `circular`, `flip`,
`drag_origin`, `drag_start_crop`, `focused_part`, `locale`, `messages`, `ids`).

**`Props`** (spec §1.4) + **added** `on_crop_change: Option<Callback<dyn
Fn(CropArea) + Send + Sync>>`. Keep `#[derive(..., HasId)]` and the `Default`
impl. Note `PartialEq` on Props with a callback field: follow SignaturePad's
exact pattern for how callbacks participate in `PartialEq` (callbacks compared by
presence/pointer per the existing convention — copy it).

**`Messages`** (spec §4.1) — verbatim, plus `impl ComponentMessages`.

**`Machine` impl**:
- `init` — per spec, `Bindable::controlled`/`uncontrolled` from `props.crop` /
  `props.default_crop`. Boots in `State::Idle`.
- `on_props_changed` (new) — assert `id` stable; push `SyncCrop` if `crop`
  changed, `SyncProps` if any of `aspect_ratio/zoom/min_zoom/max_zoom/disabled/
  circular/flip` changed (matches SignaturePad).
- `transition` — spec §1.5 logic, with these changes:
  - `Focus`/`Blur`/`SyncCrop`/`SyncProps` pass through before the `disabled`
    gate (SignaturePad ordering). `SyncCrop` sets crop + `sync_controlled`;
    `SyncProps` copies config fields from props into ctx.
  - Replace string effects with `.with_effect(PendingEffect::named(
    Effect::AnnounceCropMoved))` on `NudgeCrop` (keyboard "moved" announcement)
    and `Effect::AnnounceCropResized` on `ResizeMove`. (Spec attaches "moved" to
    `DragMove`; align announce semantics to §3.3 — "moved" after arrow-key nudge,
    "resized" after handle resize — and update spec text if it diverges.)
  - Attach `Effect::CropChange` (via a `crop_change_effect()` helper like
    `data_change_effect`) wherever user interaction mutates crop: `DragMove`,
    `ResizeMove`, `NudgeCrop`, `SetCropArea`, `SetRotation`, `Reset`. Not on
    `SyncCrop` (originates from parent).
  - `resize_crop_area` / `enforce_aspect_ratio` helpers: copy spec verbatim,
    fixing `ctx.crop.get()` → `*ctx.crop.get()` and using `.pending()` where the
    in-flight value is needed.
- `connect` — returns `Api { state, ctx, props, send }`.

**`Part`** (spec §1.6): `Root, Image, Overlay, CropArea, Grid,
Handle { position: CropHandle }, ZoomSlider, RotationSlider, ResetTrigger,
Label`. `Handle`'s field needs the `#[part(default = ...)]` attribute (check
`ComponentPart` derive for enum variants with fields — see how existing
field-carrying Part enums handle `all()`).

**`Api`** — all `*_attrs()` methods + keyboard/pointer handlers verbatim from
spec §1.6, adjusting `ctx.crop.get()` → `*ctx.crop.get()`. `impl ConnectApi`
dispatches `part_attrs`.

### Tests (TDD — write first)

1. **Inline `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`** in `mod.rs` (unit + snapshot), sections
   per SignaturePad: helpers (`test_props`, `fresh_service`, `snapshot_attrs`,
   leaked-`api_for`), data model (`CropArea::default`, `AspectRatio::as_ratio`,
   `CropResult::from_crop_area`), init (controlled vs uncontrolled), transitions
   (every spec event + the named tests: state transitions, crop positioning,
   aspect-ratio lock, drag-move, resize-from-handles, zoom clamp, rotation,
   min/max crop size = 0.05 clamp), prop-sync (`SyncCrop`/`SyncProps`),
   `CropChange`/announce effects fired, Api accessors + ARIA, `data-ars-state`.
   - **Snapshots**: cover each `Part`'s `*_attrs()` and each output-affecting
     state branch. The snapshot-count gate (3 state variants > 2) requires
     `≥ variants × min_per_variant` snapshots and caps at the parts-derived
     budget — size between those bounds (representative parts × the 3 states).
2. **Spec-conformance**: add `image_cropper_anatomy_matches_spec` to
   `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs` via
   `assert_anatomy("image-cropper", &[(Part::Root,"root"), ... ])` — note
   `Handle` expands to its 8 positions in `Part::all()`; match the derive's
   actual `all()` ordering.
3. **Proptest**: add `mod image_cropper_proptests` to
   `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs` with an
   `arb_event()` strategy and invariants — crop stays within `[0,1]`, width/
   height `≥ 0.05`, `connect()` never panics. Use
   `#![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]` and
   `#[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]`.

### Spec synchronization (same PR)
Edit `spec/components/specialized/image-cropper.md`:
- §1.2 add `SyncCrop`/`SyncProps` events.
- §1.4 add `on_crop_change` prop (+ Default `None`).
- §1.5 add the `Effect` enum, `on_props_changed`, replace string-named announce
  effects + `use_platform_effects()` with `PendingEffect::named(Effect::...)`,
  add `CropChange` effect wiring, and fix `ctx.crop.get()` deref usages.
- §3.3 / announce mapping: ensure "moved" = nudge, "resized" = handle resize.
- §5.3 update the crop-change row to reference `on_crop_change`.

## Verification
- `cargo test -p ars-components image_cropper` (unit + snapshot).
- `cargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject` then review/accept
  `.snap` fixtures (attach `snapshot-reviewed` label on the PR).
- `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance` (anatomy).
- `PROPTEST_CASES=1000 cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --ignored image_cropper`.
- `cargo doc -p ars-components` (no `missing_docs` failures).
- Run the `post-implementation-audit` skill (all three phases; land every
  finding here).
- After user approval: `cargo xci-fast`, then commit + PR to `main` with
  `Closes #296`, `snapshot-reviewed` label, then `waiting-for-codex-review`.

1	//! `SignaturePad` component state machine and connect API.
2	//!
3	//! The `SignaturePad` captures freehand pointer/touch input as vector path data.
4	//! It records strokes as point arrays (with pressure and timestamp), supports
5	//! undo and clear, and exposes the accumulated [`SignatureData`] for form
6	//! submission (as an SVG path) and export.
7	//!
8	//! The agnostic core owns the stroke data model, the drawing state machine,
9	//! `min_distance` point culling, stroke validation, and the ARIA/`data-ars-*`
10	//! attribute surface. It does **not** touch the live `<canvas>`: pointer
11	//! capture, device-pixel-ratio scaling, bounding-rect measurement, and raster
12	//! rasterization (PNG/JPEG) belong to the framework adapters.
13	//!
14	//! Like the other component machines, the core only emits typed [`Effect`]
15	//! intents; the adapter fulfils them. [`Effect::DrawingListeners`] tells the
16	//! adapter to attach global pointer-drag listeners (e.g. via
17	//! `PlatformEffects::track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch [`Event::DrawMove`] and
18	//! [`Event::DrawEnd`]; [`Effect::AnnounceProvided`] and
19	//! [`Effect::AnnounceCleared`] tell the adapter to announce the corresponding
20	//! message into a polite `aria-live` region.
21	
22	use alloc::{string::String, vec, vec::Vec};
23	use core::fmt::{self, Debug, Write as _};
24	
25	use ars_core::{
26	    AriaAttr, AttrMap, Bindable, Callback, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart,
27	    ConnectApi, Env, HasId, HtmlAttr, Locale, MessageFn, PendingEffect, RasterError, RasterImage,
28	    RasterPoint, RasterSpec, SignatureRasterizer, TransitionPlan, no_cleanup,
29	};
30	
31	/// A single point in a signature stroke.
32	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
33	pub struct SignaturePoint {
34	    /// The x coordinate of the point.
35	    pub x: f64,
36	
37	    /// The y coordinate of the point.
38	    pub y: f64,
39	
40	    /// Pressure from 0.0 to 1.0 (for pressure-sensitive input).
41	    pub pressure: f64,
42	
43	    /// Timestamp in milliseconds.
44	    pub timestamp: f64,
45	}
46	
47	/// A continuous stroke (pen-down to pen-up).
48	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
49	pub struct SignatureStroke {
50	    /// The points in the stroke.
51	    pub points: Vec<SignaturePoint>,
52	}
53	
54	/// The complete signature data.
55	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
56	pub struct SignatureData {
57	    /// The strokes in the signature.
58	    pub strokes: Vec<SignatureStroke>,
59	}
60	
61	impl SignatureData {
62	    /// Check if the signature data is empty, i.e. it has no recorded points.
63	    ///
64	    /// Emptiness is point-based, not stroke-count-based: externally supplied or
65	    /// deserialized data containing only empty strokes (no points) is treated as
66	    /// blank, matching [`to_svg_path`](Self::to_svg_path) and
67	    /// [`point_count`](Self::point_count), so it never looks like a real
68	    /// signature (guide stays visible, clear/undo stay disabled, init stays
69	    /// [`Idle`](State::Idle)).
70	    #[must_use]
71	    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
72	        self.strokes.iter().all(|stroke| stroke.points.is_empty())
73	    }
74	
75	    /// Convert to SVG path data string.
76	    ///
77	    /// Each stroke contributes a `moveto` to its first point followed by a
78	    /// `lineto` to each subsequent point. Strokes are concatenated directly,
79	    /// so the resulting path describes the whole signature as one `d` value.
80	    #[must_use]
81	    pub fn to_svg_path(&self) -> String {
82	        let mut path = String::new();
83	
84	        for stroke in &self.strokes {
85	            let Some(first) = stroke.points.first() else {
86	                continue;
87	            };
88	
89	            let _ = write!(path, "M{:.1},{:.1}", first.x, first.y);
90	
91	            for point in &stroke.points[1..] {
92	                let _ = write!(path, " L{:.1},{:.1}", point.x, point.y);
93	            }
94	        }
95	        path
96	    }
97	
98	    /// Total number of points across all strokes.
99	    #[must_use]
100	    pub fn point_count(&self) -> usize {
101	        self.strokes.iter().map(|stroke| stroke.points.len()).sum()
102	    }
103	
104	    /// Export the signature in one of the resolution-independent formats the
105	    /// agnostic core can produce.
106	    ///
107	    /// For [`SignatureFormat::Svg`] the core generates the markup from stroke
108	    /// data; for [`SignatureFormat::Points`] the raw [`SignaturePoint`] vectors
109	    /// are returned directly, enabling server-side vector reconstruction
110	    /// regardless of display resolution.
111	    ///
112	    /// Raster formats (PNG/JPEG/WebP) require a pixel surface, so they are
113	    /// produced by [`SignatureData::export_raster`] via an injected
114	    /// [`SignatureRasterizer`] rather than by this method.
115	    #[must_use]
116	    pub fn export(&self, format: SignatureFormat) -> SignatureExport {
117	        match format {
118	            SignatureFormat::Svg => SignatureExport::Svg(self.to_svg_path()),
119	
120	            SignatureFormat::Points => SignatureExport::Points(
121	                self.strokes
122	                    .iter()
123	                    .map(|stroke| stroke.points.clone())
124	                    .collect(),
125	            ),
126	        }
127	    }
128	
129	    /// Rasterize the signature into an encoded image (PNG/JPEG) via an injected
130	    /// [`SignatureRasterizer`].
131	    ///
132	    /// Raster output needs a pixel surface, which the agnostic core does not
133	    /// have — so the caller supplies the platform rasterizer (`ars-dom`'s
134	    /// `WebSignatureRasterizer` in the browser). The per-point
135	    /// [`pressure`](SignaturePoint::pressure) is forwarded to the rasterizer so
136	    /// firmer presses render as thicker strokes. Requiring the rasterizer as an
137	    /// argument keeps raster export impossible to call without a backend
138	    /// (make-invalid-states-unrepresentable) instead of panicking.
139	    ///
140	    /// # Errors
141	    ///
142	    /// Propagates the rasterizer's [`RasterError`] — notably
143	    /// [`RasterError::Unsupported`] under SSR/tests.
144	    pub fn export_raster(
145	        &self,
146	        rasterizer: &dyn SignatureRasterizer,
147	        spec: &RasterSpec,
148	    ) -> Result<RasterImage, RasterError> {
149	        let strokes = self
150	            .strokes
151	            .iter()
152	            .map(|stroke| {
153	                stroke
154	                    .points
155	                    .iter()
156	                    .map(|&SignaturePoint { x, y, pressure, .. }| RasterPoint { x, y, pressure })
157	                    .collect()
158	            })
159	            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
160	
161	        rasterizer.rasterize(&strokes, spec)
162	    }
163	}
164	
165	/// Resolution-independent export formats the agnostic core can produce.
166	///
167	/// Raster formats (PNG/JPEG/WebP) need a pixel surface and are produced by
168	/// [`SignatureData::export_raster`] via an injected [`SignatureRasterizer`], not
169	/// here — keeping this type free of variants the core cannot fulfil
170	/// (make-invalid-states-unrepresentable, so [`SignatureData::export`] is total
171	/// with no unreachable arm).
172	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
173	pub enum SignatureFormat {
174	    /// SVG path markup.
175	    Svg,
176	
177	    /// Raw point data.
178	    Points,
179	}
180	
181	/// Exported signature data in one of the [`SignatureFormat`] variants.
182	///
183	/// Mirrors [`SignatureFormat`]: every variant here is something the core can
184	/// actually return. Adapters define their own export type for raster output.
185	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
186	pub enum SignatureExport {
187	    /// SVG markup string.
188	    Svg(String),
189	
190	    /// Raw point data for vector reconstruction.
191	    /// Each inner `Vec` is one continuous stroke.
192	    Points(Vec<Vec<SignaturePoint>>),
193	}
194	
195	/// The states for the `SignaturePad` component.
196	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
197	pub enum State {
198	    /// No strokes, canvas is blank.
199	    Idle,
200	
201	    /// The user is actively drawing (pointer/touch down).
202	    Drawing,
203	
204	    /// At least one stroke has been completed.
205	    Completed,
206	}
207	
208	/// The events for the `SignaturePad` component.
209	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
210	pub enum Event {
211	    /// Pointer/touch down on the canvas.
212	    DrawStart {
213	        /// The x coordinate of the draw start.
214	        x: f64,
215	
216	        /// The y coordinate of the draw start.
217	        y: f64,
218	
219	        /// The pressure of the draw start.
220	        pressure: f64,
221	    },
222	
223	    /// Pointer/touch move while drawing.
224	    DrawMove {
225	        /// The x coordinate of the draw move.
226	        x: f64,
227	
228	        /// The y coordinate of the draw move.
229	        y: f64,
230	
231	        /// The pressure of the draw move.
232	        pressure: f64,
233	    },
234	
235	    /// Pointer/touch up, ending a stroke.
236	    DrawEnd,
237	
238	    /// Undo the last stroke.
239	    Undo,
240	
241	    /// Clear all strokes.
242	    Clear,
243	
244	    /// Focus entered the canvas.
245	    Focus,
246	
247	    /// Focus left the canvas.
248	    Blur,
249	
250	    /// The controlled [`Props::data`] changed; re-sync the bound signature data.
251	    /// Dispatched by [`Machine::on_props_changed`](ars_core::Machine::on_props_changed),
252	    /// not by user interaction, and processed regardless of disabled/read-only.
253	    SyncData,
254	
255	    /// A configuration prop (disabled, read-only, pen color/width, min distance)
256	    /// changed; mirror the new values into the context. Dispatched by
257	    /// [`Machine::on_props_changed`](ars_core::Machine::on_props_changed), and
258	    /// processed regardless of disabled/read-only so the pad can be re-enabled.
259	    SyncProps,
260	}
261	
262	/// The context for the `SignaturePad` component.
263	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
264	pub struct Context {
265	    /// The accumulated signature data.
266	    pub data: Bindable<SignatureData>,
267	
268	    /// The stroke currently being drawn (None if not drawing).
269	    pub current_stroke: Option<SignatureStroke>,
270	
271	    /// Whether the pad is disabled.
272	    pub disabled: bool,
273	
274	    /// Whether the pad is read-only (shows existing signature but cannot modify).
275	    pub readonly: bool,
276	
277	    /// Stroke color (CSS color string).
278	    pub pen_color: String,
279	
280	    /// Stroke width in pixels.
281	    pub pen_width: f64,
282	
283	    /// Minimum distance between points to record (for performance).
284	    pub min_distance: f64,
285	
286	    /// Whether the canvas has focus.
287	    pub focused: bool,
288	
289	    /// Locale for internationalized messages.
290	    pub locale: Locale,

948	    /// Root element attributes.
949	    #[must_use]
950	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
951	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
952	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
953	
954	        attrs
955	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id())
956	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
957	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
958	            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_token());
959	
960	        if self.ctx.disabled {
961	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true);
962	        }
963	
964	        if self.ctx.readonly {
965	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-readonly"), true);
966	        }
967	
968	        attrs
969	    }
970	
971	    /// Canvas element attributes.
972	    #[must_use]
973	    pub fn canvas_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
974	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
975	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Canvas.data_attrs();
976	
977	        attrs
978	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("canvas"))
979	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
980	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
981	            .set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, "0")
982	            .set(HtmlAttr::Class, "ars-touch-none");
983	
984	        // `application` opts the canvas out of the screen-reader virtual cursor
985	        // so it can capture raw pointer/touch input; a non-interactive pad is a
986	        // static image instead.
987	        if self.ctx.readonly || self.ctx.disabled {
988	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "img");
989	        } else {
990	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "application");
991	        }
992	
993	        attrs
994	            .set(
995	                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),
996	                (self.ctx.messages.canvas_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
997	            )
998	            .set(
999	                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy),
1000	                self.ctx.ids.part("label"),
1001	            );
1002	
1003	        if self.ctx.disabled {
1004	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), "true");
1005	        }
1006	
1007	        if self.ctx.readonly {
1008	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly), "true");
1009	        }
1010	
1011	        if self.ctx.focused {
1012	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-focus-visible"), true);
1013	        }
1014	
1015	        attrs
1016	    }

1101	    /// Hidden-input attributes. The value is the signature's SVG path data,
1102	    /// suitable for form submission.

1104	    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
1105	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
1106	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();
1107	
1108	        attrs
1109	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
1110	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
1111	            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
1112	
1113	        if let Some(name) = &self.props.name {
1114	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name.clone());
1115	        }
1116	
1117	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, self.ctx.data.get().to_svg_path());
1118	
1119	        // A disabled control is excluded from form submission; mirror that on
1120	        // the hidden input so a disabled pad cannot submit stale signature data.
1121	        if self.ctx.disabled {
1122	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
1123	        }
1124	
1125	        attrs
1126	    }

pub struct KeyboardEventData {
    /// The named key, normalized from W3C `KeyboardEvent.key` values.

    /// The printable character produced by this key press, if any.
    ///
    /// For example, `A` maps to `Some('a')` or `Some('A')`, `Space` maps to
    /// `Some(' ')`, and named control keys such as `Enter` map to `None`.
    pub character: Option<char>,

    /// The physical key code, such as `"KeyA"` or `"Digit1"`.
    ///
    /// This uses the browser `KeyboardEvent.code` contract rather than the
    /// localized `KeyboardEvent.key` value.
    pub code: String,

    /// Whether the Shift modifier was held.
    pub shift_key: bool,

    /// Whether the physical Ctrl key was held.
    ///
    /// This is the raw Ctrl state, not a platform-abstracted action modifier.
    /// Consumers that want Ctrl on Windows/Linux and Cmd on macOS should use
    /// the higher-level action-key abstractions provided elsewhere.
    pub ctrl_key: bool,

    /// Whether the Alt modifier was held.
    pub alt_key: bool,

    /// Whether the Meta modifier was held.
    pub meta_key: bool,

    /// Whether this event is an auto-repeat generated by holding the key down.
    pub repeat: bool,

    /// Whether this event fired during active IME composition.
    ///

fn find_scope_attr(attrs: &[syn::Attribute]) -> syn::Result<LitStr> {
    for attr in attrs {
        if !attr.path().is_ident("scope") {
            continue;
        }

        let Meta::NameValue(name_value) = &attr.meta else {
            break;
        };

        let Expr::Lit(expr_lit) = &name_value.value else {
            break;
        };

        let Lit::Str(scope) = &expr_lit.lit else {
            break;
        };

        return Ok(scope.clone());
    }

    Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(
        attrs
            .first()
            .map_or_else(TokenStream::new, quote::ToTokens::to_token_stream),
        "ComponentPart requires a #[scope = \"kebab-case-name\"] attribute",
    ))
}

fn validate_root_variant(data: &syn::DataEnum) -> syn::Result<&Variant> {
    let Some(first_variant) = data.variants.first() else {
        return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(
            data.enum_token,
            "ComponentPart requires a first unit variant to use as ROOT",
        ));
    };

    if !matches!(first_variant.fields, Fields::Unit) {
        return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(
            first_variant,
            "ComponentPart requires the first variant to be a unit variant",
        ));
    }

    Ok(first_variant)
}

fn collect_field_types(data: &syn::DataEnum) -> Vec<Type> {
    data.variants
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|variant| match &variant.fields {
            Fields::Unit => Vec::new(),
            Fields::Unnamed(fields) => fields
                .unnamed
                .iter()
                .map(|field| field.ty.clone())
                .collect(),
            Fields::Named(fields) => fields.named.iter().map(|field| field.ty.clone()).collect(),
        })
        .collect()
}
```

222	fn collect_defaultless_field_types(data: &syn::DataEnum) -> syn::Result<Vec<Type>> {
223	    let mut types = Vec::new();
224	
225	    for variant in &data.variants {
226	        match &variant.fields {
227	            Fields::Unit => {}
228	
229	            Fields::Unnamed(fields) => {
230	                for field in &fields.unnamed {
231	                    if field_default_expr(field)?.is_none() {
232	                        types.push(field.ty.clone());
233	                    }
234	                }
235	            }
236	
237	            Fields::Named(fields) => {
238	                for field in &fields.named {
239	                    if field_default_expr(field)?.is_none() {
240	                        types.push(field.ty.clone());
241	                    }
242	                }
243	            }
244	        }
245	    }
246	
247	    Ok(types)
248	}
249	
250	fn with_field_bounds(
251	    generics: &Generics,
252	    field_types: &[Type],
253	    bounds: &[TokenStream],
254	) -> Generics {
255	    let mut generics = generics.clone();
256	
257	    let where_clause = generics.make_where_clause();
258	
259	    for field_ty in field_types {
260	        for bound in bounds {
261	            where_clause
262	                .predicates
263	                .push(parse_quote!(#field_ty: #bound));
264	        }
265	    }
266	
267	    generics
268	}
269	
270	fn name_arm(variant: &Variant) -> TokenStream {
271	    let ident = &variant.ident;
272	    let name = LitStr::new(&ident.to_string().to_kebab_case(), ident.span());
273	
274	    match &variant.fields {
275	        Fields::Unit => quote!(Self::#ident => #name),
276	        Fields::Unnamed(_) => quote!(Self::#ident(..) => #name),
277	        Fields::Named(_) => quote!(Self::#ident { .. } => #name),
278	    }
279	}
280	
281	fn all_value(variant: &Variant) -> syn::Result<TokenStream> {
282	    let ident = &variant.ident;
283	
284	    Ok(match &variant.fields {
285	        Fields::Unit => quote!(Self::#ident),
286	
287	        Fields::Unnamed(fields) => {
288	            let defaults = fields
289	                .unnamed
290	                .iter()
291	                .map(field_all_expr)
292	                .collect::<syn::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;
293	
294	            quote!(Self::#ident(#(#defaults),*))
295	        }
296	
297	        Fields::Named(fields) => {
298	            let defaults = fields
299	                .named
300	                .iter()
301	                .map(|field| -> syn::Result<TokenStream> {
302	                    let ident = field.ident.as_ref().expect("named field");
303	                    let expr = field_all_expr(field)?;
304	
305	                    Ok(quote!(#ident: #expr))
306	                })
307	                .collect::<syn::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;
308	
309	            quote!(Self::#ident { #(#defaults),* })
310	        }
311	    })
312	}
313	
314	fn field_all_expr(field: &Field) -> syn::Result<TokenStream> {
315	    Ok(if let Some(expr) = field_default_expr(field)? {
316	        quote!(#expr)
317	    } else {
318	        quote!(::core::default::Default::default())
319	    })
320	}
321	
322	fn field_default_expr(field: &Field) -> syn::Result<Option<Expr>> {
323	    let mut default = None;
324	
325	    for attr in &field.attrs {
326	        if !attr.path().is_ident("part") {
327	            continue;
328	        }
329	
330	        attr.parse_nested_meta(|meta| {
331	            if meta.path.is_ident("default") {
332	                let value = meta.value()?;
333	                let expr = value.parse::<Expr>()?;
334	
335	                if default.replace(expr).is_some() {
336	                    return Err(meta.error("ComponentPart accepts only one field default"));
337	                }
338	
339	                Ok(())
