  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn pointer_and_reset_dispatchers_emit_events() {\n        let log: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Event>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let sink = Arc::clone(&log);\n        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(test_props()));\n        let (_, ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default());\n        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));\n        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(State::Idle));\n        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(move |event: Event| {\n            sink.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n        }));\n        let api = Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        };\n\n        api.on_crop_area_pointer_down(0.3, 0.4);\n        api.on_handle_pointer_down(CropHandle::Top, 0.5, 0.1);\n        api.on_reset();\n\n        let events = log.lock().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(events[0], Event::DragStart { x: 0.3, y: 0.4 });\n        assert_eq!(\n            events[1],\n            Event::ResizeStart {\n                handle: CropHandle::Top,\n                x: 0.5,\n                y: 0.1,\n            }\n        );\n        assert_eq!(events[2], Event::Reset);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn part_attrs_dispatches_every_part() {\n        let api = Fixture::default().api();\n        // Every anatomy part dispatches through `ConnectApi::part_attrs` without\n        // panicking and emits the component scope marker.\n        for part in Part::all() {\n            let attrs = api.part_attrs(part);\n            assert_eq!(\n                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-scope\")).map(|value| value),\n                Some(\"image-cropper\")\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── snapshots ─────────────────────────"
}

> AGENT

Implementation and the mandatory three-phase audit are complete. Here is the result for your review.

## Task #296 — ImageCropper agnostic core

### What landed
- **New module** [crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs](crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs) — the full stateful machine (`Idle`/`Dragging`/`Resizing`), crop geometry/aspect-ratio/zoom/rotation/flip math, keyboard intent, and the 10-part `ConnectApi` attribute surface. Wired into [specialized/mod.rs](crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs).
- **64 unit + snapshot tests** inline, **19 committed `.snap` fixtures**, **1 spec-conformance test**, **1 proptest** (geometry-invariant fuzzing).

### Spec drift reconciled in-PR (per your "add full sync" decision)
The spec's §1.5 was written against an older engine and couldn't compile. Both the impl and [spec/components/specialized/image-cropper.md](spec/components/specialized/image-cropper.md) were brought into agreement:

| Drift | Resolution |
|---|---|
| String-named effects + `use_platform_effects()` in core | Typed `Effect` enum (`AnnounceCropMoved`/`AnnounceCropResized`/`CropChange`), dispatched by adapter |
| `Bindable::get()` returns `&T` | `*ctx.crop.pending()` in mutating closures |
| `KeyboardKey::Character('+')` (doesn't exist) | match on `data.character: Option<char>` |
| Controlled `crop` had no notify/sync path | Added `on_crop_change` prop + `SyncCrop`/`SyncProps` events + `on_props_changed` + `CropChange` effect (matches SignaturePad) |
| `CropHandle` missing `Hash` (needed for `Part::Handle` field) | added `Hash` |
| Announce semantics | "moved" on nudge + drag, "resized" on resize (§3.3) |

Added for RTL correctness: `on_crop_area_keydown` takes an `is_rtl` flag so `ArrowRight` always moves toward the inline-end.

### Audit findings (all landed, none deferred)
- **Phase 1 (drift):** reconciled during the spec-sync step above; confirmed component specs intentionally omit builders/type-aliases/named-consts (matches tabs/tree_view).
- **Phase 2 (2 clean rounds):** adapter specs are high-level sketches already using `api.root_attrs()` with no stale refs; catalog registered; no feature wiring/deps/foundation-promotion needed.
- **Phase 3 (coverage):** added tests closing every gap — all 8 handle resize arms, aspect-ratio overflow re-clamp (both fns), `_ => None` catch-all, `CropOutputFormat`, `connect()`/Debug/accessors, `part_attrs` dispatch, full keyboard maps. Only 2 lines uncovered: an unreachable `if let`-else defensive guard and a test `panic!` guard.

### Verification
- `cargo test -p ars-components` → **5256 + 126 + 27 + 4 pass, 0 fail**
- `cargo clippy -p ars-components --all-targets --all-features -D warnings` → clean
- `cargo doc` → image_cropper clean
- `cargo xtask spec validate` (340 files) + spec snippet compile → pass
- `cargo insta test --features i18n --unreferenced=reject` → no orphans

Per the workflow I have **not** committed. On your approval I'll run `cargo xci-fast`, then commit and open a PR to `main` with `Closes #296` + the `snapshot-reviewed` label, then run the Codex-review loop.

> AGENT

Verified the key facts:

- **`Part` is `Clone` but not `Copy`** (the `ComponentPart` derive emits a hand-written `Clone`, no `Copy`). Putting it in `Event::Focus` makes `Event` lose its `Copy` derive — though that's not unprecedented (`grid_list::Event` is already non-`Copy` because it carries `Option<Key>`).
- **`Part` is a loose fit for focus**: only 4 of its 10 variants are focusable (`CropArea`, `ZoomSlider`, `RotationSlider`, `ResetTrigger`). `Focus { part: Part::Overlay }` or `Focus { part: Part::Handle { .. } }` would be expressible but meaningless — which actually *re-introduces* invalid states, cutting against the "make invalid states unrepresentable" principle.
- Today `focused_part` is consumed in exactly one place: `crop_area_attrs` sets `ars-focus-visible` when it `== Some("crop-area")` — a magic string that must match the part's kebab name.

So yes, it's possible, but `Part` itself isn't the best target. The cleanest type-safe upgrade is a small dedicated enum of only the focusable parts. This is a public-API + spec change, so I'd like your call on direction.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Done. All green after the change.

## Change applied: `Focus`/`Blur` now carry `Part`

Your instinct was correct — `Part` *can* be `Copy`. The blocker was only that the shared `ComponentPart` derive emits a hand-written `Clone` (no `Copy`), and it can't emit `Copy` workspace-wide because other `Part` enums carry non-`Copy` fields (`Key`, `SafeUrl`, calendar dates). But a **local `impl Copy for Part {}`** works here since every field is `Copy` (unit variants + `Handle`'s `CropHandle`), and clippy stays clean (it skips the proc-macro-generated `Clone` span, so no `expl_impl_clone_on_copy`).

What changed (impl + spec in lockstep):
- `Event::Focus { part: Part }` / `Blur { part: Part }` — was `&'static str`. `Event` keeps its `Copy` derive.
- `Context::focused_part: Option<Part>`.
- `crop_area_attrs`: `self.ctx.focused_part == Some(Part::CropArea)` — the magic `"crop-area"` string is gone; the comparison now type-checks against the real anatomy.
- `impl Copy for Part {}` added with a comment explaining why.
- Tests + the `Fixture` updated to `Part::CropArea`; spec §1.2/§1.3/§1.6 updated to match.

Net: focus targets are now type-safe and tied to the anatomy enum, with no `Copy` regression on `Event`.

Verification (re-run after the change):
- `cargo clippy -p ars-components --all-targets --all-features -D warnings` → clean
- `cargo test -p ars-components` → 5256 + 126 + 27 + 4, **0 failures**
- `cargo xtask spec validate` (340 files) + spec snippet compile → pass
- `cargo insta test --features i18n --unreferenced=reject` → no orphans

One tradeoff to flag honestly: `Part` has 6 non-focusable variants (`Overlay`, `Image`, `Grid`, `Handle`, `Label`, and the sliders are focusable but `Overlay`/`Image`/`Grid`/`Handle` aren't), so `Focus { part: Part::Overlay }` is now *expressible* though meaningless — it simply won't match `Some(Part::CropArea)`. This is the price of reusing the anatomy enum over a bespoke focusable-only enum; given DRY and a single source of anatomy truth, it's a reasonable call.

Still no commit — awaiting your go-ahead to run `cargo xci-fast`, open the PR (`Closes #296`, `snapshot-reviewed` label), and start the Codex-review loop.

> AGENT

So adding `Copy` to the derive list should be equivalent to my manual impl, just more idiomatic. Let me verify it compiles cleanly.

> AGENT

Done — switched to `#[derive(ComponentPart, Copy)]`, which is cleaner than the separate `impl`.

It works because `#[derive(Copy)]` only emits `impl Copy for Part {}`, and `Copy`'s sole prerequisite — a `Clone` impl — is already supplied by `ComponentPart` (plus all fields are `Copy`). The two derives compose: `ComponentPart` owns `Clone`/`Debug`/`PartialEq`/`Eq`/`Hash`, and `Copy` rides on top.

Note this only works for *this* `Part` because all its fields are `Copy`. The `ComponentPart` derive itself can't add `Copy` unconditionally — `Part` enums elsewhere carry non-`Copy` fields (`Key`, `SafeUrl`, calendar dates) — which is why it stays opt-in per-component via the derive list.

Removed the standalone `impl Copy for Part {}` from both the module and the spec. Verified:
- `cargo clippy -p ars-components --lib --all-features` → clean
- `cargo test -p ars-components --lib image_cropper` → 64 pass
- `cargo xtask spec validate` + spec snippet compile → pass

Still uncommitted, awaiting your go-ahead for `cargo xci-fast` → PR (`Closes #296`, `snapshot-reviewed`) → Codex-review loop.

> AGENT

No — and the reason is a real architectural asymmetry, not an oversight. I checked how SignaturePad's rasterizer is actually wired:

**SignaturePad:** `SignatureRasterizer::rasterize(strokes, spec)` ([raster.rs:205](crates/ars-core/src/raster.rs#L205)) takes **core-owned vector data** — the strokes. The agnostic core *possesses* the source (point arrays), so it can hand them to an injected backend that only adds a pixel surface + encoder. The trait exists because the core has data it can't rasterize itself.

**ImageCropper:** the core never possesses the source pixels — it only holds a `src` *URL string*. The crop is fully described by the platform-neutral **`CropResult`** (x/y/w/h + rotation + scale + flip + aspect), which I already expose via `Api::result()`. The actual crop is: adapter takes its live `<img>`/canvas + that `CropResult` → `ctx.drawImage(...)` → `canvas.toBlob(format)`. There is no core-owned input to feed a rasterizer, and any such trait would have to be generic over a platform image handle (`HtmlImageElement` on web, a decoded buffer natively), which would leak platform types into the agnostic core — the opposite of what the trait is for.

Both the issue and the adapter specs confirm this split:
- Issue #296: *"…raster export belong in adapters via native handles and supplied metrics."*
- [leptos-components/.../image-cropper.md:57](spec/leptos-components/specialized/image-cropper.md#L57) / dioxus: export is *"client-only"*, *"optional adapter-owned capability"*, with a *fallback path* — never a core-injected backend.

So the core's complete, platform-neutral contribution to export is the two types it already has:
- **`CropResult`** — the geometry/transform recipe (`Api::result()`).
- **`CropOutputFormat`** — the desired encoding (Png/Jpeg/WebP + quality), the analog of SignaturePad's `RasterFormat`.

The adapter consumes both at export time to do the pixel work against its live handle. That's the adapter task (out of scope for #296, which is agnostic-core-only). **Nothing to add to this PR** — adding a rasterizer trait here would either be dead (no pixels to rasterize) or force platform handle types into `ars-core`.

The only loose end is cosmetic: `CropOutputFormat` is currently a core type with no in-core consumer (a unit test exercises its variants). That's intentional and mirrors how `RasterFormat`/`RasterSpec` live in `ars-core` as the shared, framework-neutral vocabulary the adapters import — so I'd keep it as-is.

{

Stateful crop tool with `Idle`/`Dragging`/`Resizing` states, normalized crop geometry, aspect-ratio enforcement, min-crop-size floor, zoom, rotation, flip, keyboard nudging, and the 10-part `ConnectApi` attribute surface.\n\nRaster export stays an adapter concern — the agnostic core only owns the `CropResult` recipe (`Api::result()`) and the `CropOutputFormat` encoding vocabulary; the adapter applies them to its live image handle (`drawImage` + `toBlob`). This matches the issue's element/ref note and both adapter specs (\"export ... optional adapter-owned capability\"). Unlike SignaturePad, the core never possesses the source pixels, so no rasterizer trait belongs here.\n\n## Tests\n- 64 unit + snapshot tests, **19 committed insta fixtures** (every part × output-affecting state/prop branch)\n- spec-conformance anatomy test (`image_cropper_anatomy_matches_spec`)\n- geometry-invariant proptest (nightly `extended-proptest`)\n- Coverage: only two unreachable lines uncovered (an `if let` else guard + a test `panic!`)\n\n## Spec synchronization (landed in this PR)\nThe spec's §1.5 was written against an older engine and could not compile; both sides were reconciled (spec-first rule):\n- Typed `Effect` enum (`AnnounceCropMoved`/`AnnounceCropResized`/`CropChange`) dispatched by the adapter — replaces string-named effects + the core-side `use_platform_effects()` call.\n- `Bindable::get()` returns `&T` → mutating closures read `*pending()`.\n- Keyboard shortcuts read `data.character` (`KeyboardKey` has no printable variant).\n- Controlled `crop` gains a real sync path: `on_crop_change` callback + `SyncCrop`/`SyncProps` events + `on_props_changed` (matches SignaturePad/ColorPicker).\n- `Focus`/`Blur` carry the `Part` enum (made `Copy` via the derive list) instead of a `&'static str` token.\n- `CropHandle` gains `Hash` (required as a `Part::Handle` field).\n\n## Verification\n- `cargo xci-fast` — all 10 gates pass (fmt, clippy, unit, integration, adapter, spec-compile-snippets, error-variant-coverage, mutual-exclusion, snapshot-count, coverage-native)\n- `post-implementation-audit` ran all three phases; every finding landed here.\n\nCloses #296\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -3",

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7X0I
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1052
parent_db_id: 3352706048
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Use the configured default on reset**

When consumers pass a non-default `default_crop`, `Reset` currently ignores it and always writes `CropArea::default()`. That makes the reset trigger restore the library's hard-coded 10% inset instead of the configured starting crop, so any cropper initialized with a custom default cannot return to its declared default state.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7X0K
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:716
parent_db_id: 3352706050
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Keep aspect-locked crops above the minimum size**

With a fixed wide aspect ratio, collapsing a resize handle clamps `width` to the 5% minimum and then derives `height = width / ratio`; for `Wide16x9`, this yields about 2.8%, below the documented 5% minimum crop dimension. This breaks the geometry invariant for aspect-locked resizing and can leave handles/crop areas too small for subsequent interaction.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7X0O
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:810
parent_db_id: 3352706055
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Clamp initial zoom to its bounds**

When `Props::zoom` is outside `min_zoom..=max_zoom`, initialization stores the out-of-range value directly even though later `SetZoom` and prop-sync paths clamp it. A cropper created with `zoom(10.0).max_zoom(3.0)` therefore first renders with `aria-valuenow="10.00"` and CSS zoom 10 while advertising `aria-valuemax="3.00"`, until some later zoom update corrects it.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7X0Q
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1031
parent_db_id: 3352706059
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Constrain rotation before updating the slider value**

The rotation slider declares `aria-valuemin="-180"` and `aria-valuemax="180"`, but `SetRotation` accepts any value and stores it unchanged. Pressing `r` three times (or dispatching `SetRotation(270.0)`) makes `rotation_slider_attrs()` emit `aria-valuenow="270"`, which is outside the advertised slider range and leaves assistive technology with an invalid value.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7X0U
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:803
parent_db_id: 3352706064
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Apply the aspect ratio during initialization**

When `Props::aspect_ratio` is set at mount, the initial controlled/default crop is stored as-is and `enforce_aspect_ratio` is not run until a later `SetAspectRatio` or prop-sync event. For example, `default_crop` with `width=0.8,height=0.4` and `aspect_ratio(Square)` first renders and reports a non-square crop despite the active constraint.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7X0V
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1490
parent_db_id: 3352706066
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Ignore composing text in root shortcuts**

`KeyboardEventData` marks printable keys produced during IME composition with `is_composing`, but this handler dispatches shortcuts from `character` without checking it. If the cropper root has focus while a user is composing text and an intermediate composed key is `r`, `h`, `v`, `+`, or `-`, the cropper can rotate/flip/zoom even though character-driven interactions should be suppressed until composition ends.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.
```

crop.height).abs() < 1e-9);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_keydown_ignores_ime_composition() {\n        let log: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Event>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let sink = Arc::clone(&log);\n        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(test_props()));\n        let (_, ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default());\n        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));\n        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(State::Idle));\n        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(move |event: Event| {\n            sink.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n        }));\n        let api = Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        };\n\n        let composing = KeyboardEventData {\n            is_composing: true,\n            ..char_key('r')\n        };\n        api.on_root_keydown(&composing);\n\n        assert!(log.lock().unwrap().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn crop_area_keydown_ignores_ime_composition() {\n        let log: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Event>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let sink = Arc::clone(&log);\n        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(test_props()));\n        let (_, ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default());\n        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));\n        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(State::Idle));\n        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(move |event: Event| {\n            sink.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n        }));\n        let api = Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        };\n\n        let composing = KeyboardEventData {\n            is_composing: true,\n            ..key(KeyboardKey::ArrowRight)\n        };\n        api.on_crop_area_keydown(&composing, false, false);\n\n        assert!(log.lock().unwrap().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── snapshots ─────────────────────────"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "        // Enforce aspect ratio if set, re-clamping height against the bottom\n        // edge so the constrained crop never overflows the image.\n        if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n            crop.height = crop.width / ratio;\n\n            if crop.y + crop.height > 1.0 {\n                crop.height = 1.0 - crop.y;\n                crop.width = crop.height * ratio;\n            }\n        }\n\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.\nfn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n        let mut crop = *ctx.crop.pending();\n\n        crop.height = crop.width / ratio;\n\n        if crop.y + crop.height > 1.0 {\n            crop.height = 1.0 - crop.y;\n            crop.width = crop.height * ratio;\n        }\n\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "        if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n            constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n        }\n\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Re-shape `crop` to the given width:height `ratio`, keeping both dimensions\n/// within `[MIN_CROP_SIZE, image edge]`.\n///\n/// The width is the driving dimension: it is held at or above the floor that\n/// keeps *both* sides `>= MIN_CROP_SIZE` (`max(MIN, MIN * ratio)`) and at or\n/// below the largest width the right and bottom edges allow, then the height is\n/// derived from it. `.max().min()` (rather than `f64::clamp`) is deliberate —\n/// when the crop sits so close to an edge that the lower and upper bounds cross,\n/// fitting inside the image wins over the minimum-size floor instead of\n/// panicking.\nfn constrain_to_ratio(crop: &mut CropArea, ratio: f64) {\n    let lower = MIN_CROP_SIZE.max(MIN_CROP_SIZE * ratio);\n    let upper = (1.0 - crop.x).min((1.0 - crop.y) * ratio);\n\n    let width = crop.width.max(lower).min(upper);\n    crop.width = width;\n    crop.height = width / ratio;\n}\n\n/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.\nfn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n        let mut crop = *ctx.crop.pending();\n        constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Normalize a rotation in degrees into the `(-180, 180]`-ish slider range the\n/// rotation control advertises, so repeated `r`/`R` rotations wrap instead of\n/// running past the declared `aria-valuemin`/`aria-valuemax`.\nfn normalize_rotation(degrees: f64) -> f64 {\n    (degrees + 180.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0\n}"
}

868	
869	    fn transition(
870	        state: &Self::State,
871	        event: &Self::Event,
872	        ctx: &Self::Context,
873	        props: &Self::Props,
874	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
875	        // Focus/Blur and the prop-sync events always pass through, regardless of
876	        // the disabled gate (prop sync must be able to re-enable the cropper).
877	        match event {
878	            Event::Focus { part } => {
879	                let part = *part;
880	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
881	                    ctx.focused_part = Some(part);
882	                }));
883	            }
884	
885	            Event::Blur { .. } => {
886	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {
887	                    ctx.focused_part = None;
888	                }));
889	            }
890	
891	            Event::SyncCrop => {
892	                let new_crop = props.crop;
893	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
894	                    if let Some(crop) = new_crop {
895	                        ctx.crop.set(crop);
896	                    }
897	
898	                    ctx.crop.sync_controlled(new_crop);
899	                }));
900	            }
901

> AGENT

Let me reconsider the `SyncCrop` enforce: `SyncCrop` only fires in controlled mode, where `Bindable::get()` returns the parent-owned controlled slot (not the constrained internal), so enforcing there is inert and misleading. Controlled values are the parent's responsibility (and the parent receives already-constrained values via `on_crop_change`). I'll revert that one.

  "new_string": "            (_, Event::Reset) => {\n                // Reset returns to the *configured* initial state — the\n                // `default_crop`, initial `zoom`, and initial `flip` the\n                // consumer declared — not the library's hard-coded defaults.\n                let default_crop = props.default_crop;\n                let zoom = props.zoom.clamp(props.min_zoom, props.max_zoom);\n                let flip = props.flip;\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.crop.set(default_crop);\n                            ctx.zoom = zoom;\n                            ctx.flip = flip;\n                            ctx.drag_origin = None;\n                            ctx.drag_start_crop = None;\n                            // Keep the reset crop consistent with an active ratio.\n                            enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx);\n                        })\n                        .with_effect(crop_change_effect()),\n                )\n            }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/spec/components/specialized/image-cropper.md",
  "old_string": "        // Enforce aspect ratio if set\n        if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n            crop.height = crop.width / ratio;\n            // Re-clamp after aspect ratio enforcement\n            if crop.y + crop.height > 1.0 {\n                crop.height = 1.0 - crop.y;\n                crop.width = crop.height * ratio;\n            }\n        }\n\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.\nfn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n        // Build on the *pending* (internal) crop: in controlled mode `get()`\n        // returns the stale parent value until it round-trips through\n        // `on_crop_change`.\n        let mut crop = *ctx.crop.pending();\n        crop.height = crop.width / ratio;\n        if crop.y + crop.height > 1.0 {\n            crop.height = 1.0 - crop.y;\n            crop.width = crop.height * ratio;\n        }\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "        if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n            constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n        }\n\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Re-shape `crop` to the given width:height `ratio`, keeping both dimensions\n/// within `[MIN_CROP_SIZE, image edge]`. The width drives the result: it is\n/// held at or above the floor that keeps *both* sides `>= MIN_CROP_SIZE`\n/// (`max(MIN, MIN * ratio)`) and at or below what the right/bottom edges allow,\n/// then the height is derived. `.max().min()` (not `f64::clamp`) is deliberate:\n/// when the crop sits so close to an edge that the bounds cross, fitting the\n/// image wins over the minimum-size floor instead of panicking.\nfn constrain_to_ratio(crop: &mut CropArea, ratio: f64) {\n    let lower = MIN_CROP_SIZE.max(MIN_CROP_SIZE * ratio);\n    let upper = (1.0 - crop.x).min((1.0 - crop.y) * ratio);\n    let width = crop.width.max(lower).min(upper);\n    crop.width = width;\n    crop.height = width / ratio;\n}\n\n/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.\nfn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n        // Build on the *pending* (internal) crop: in controlled mode `get()`\n        // returns the stale parent value until it round-trips through\n        // `on_crop_change`.\n        let mut crop = *ctx.crop.pending();\n        constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Normalize a rotation in degrees into the `[-180, 180]` range the rotation\n/// slider advertises, so repeated `r`/`R` rotations wrap instead of running\n/// past the declared `aria-valuemin`/`aria-valuemax`.\nfn normalize_rotation(degrees: f64) -> f64 {\n    (degrees + 180.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0\n}"
}

989	    pub fn on_root_keydown(&self, data: &KeyboardEventData) {

> AGENT

I've addressed all 6 Codex findings (TDD: 8 new failing tests written first, then fixed, plus a strengthened proptest invariant), synced the spec, and `cargo xci-fast` is re-running in the background. While it runs, here's the summary of what each fix did — I'll commit, reply+resolve the threads, and re-trigger Codex once the gate is green.

| # | Codex finding | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Reset ignores configured `default_crop` | `Reset` now restores `props.default_crop` + initial `zoom` (clamped) + `flip`, not hardcoded defaults |
| 2 | Aspect-locked resize drops below 5% | New `constrain_to_ratio` floors **both** dims at `max(MIN, MIN·ratio)` while preserving ratio + bounds |
| 3 | Initial zoom not clamped | `init` clamps `zoom` to `min_zoom..=max_zoom` |
| 4 | `SetRotation` exceeds slider range | `normalize_rotation` wraps into `[-180, 180]` |
| 5 | Aspect ratio not applied at init | `init` runs `enforce_aspect_ratio`; `SetCropArea` enforces too |
| 6 | IME composition fires shortcuts | both keydown handlers early-return on `data.is_composing` |

All judgment calls (reset-to-configured, rotation *wrap* vs clamp, enforcing aspect on `SetCropArea`) I'll note in the inline replies. I deliberately did **not** enforce on `SyncCrop` — it only fires in controlled mode where `Bindable::get()` returns the parent's authoritative value, so enforcement there would be inert/misleading. Standing by for the gate.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7o26
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:895
parent_db_id: 3352802852
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Re-apply the aspect constraint when syncing controlled crops**

When only the controlled `crop` prop changes while an aspect ratio such as `Square` remains unchanged, this path copies the parent value directly and never calls `enforce_aspect_ratio`. That leaves `ctx.crop` and all rendered crop-area/result geometry violating the active constraint until some unrelated aspect/resize event occurs, even though init, `SetCropArea`, and `SyncProps` all enforce it.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7o27
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:822
parent_db_id: 3352802853
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Normalize zoom bounds before clamping**

If a consumer supplies `min_zoom > max_zoom` (or a NaN bound), `f64::clamp` panics during initialization, and the same pattern is used for prop sync/reset/zoom updates. Because these are public props, a misconfigured cropper can take down the app instead of falling back to a safe ordered range or rejecting the config explicitly.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7o29
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:926
parent_db_id: 3352802855
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Keep user zoom/flip when unrelated props change**

When a parent toggles an unrelated prop such as `disabled` or `circular`, `SyncProps` still overwrites the current user-edited `zoom` and `flip` with the initial prop values. For example, after the user zooms in or presses `h`, disabling the cropper resets those edits even though `zoom`/`flip` did not change in props; only changed fields (or explicitly controlled fields) should be mirrored here.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7o2-
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:156
parent_db_id: 3352802858
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Reject invalid fixed aspect ratios**

`AspectRatio::Fixed` accepts any `f64`, but values like `0.0`, negative numbers, or NaN flow into `constrain_to_ratio` and then divide by that ratio, producing infinite, negative, or NaN crop dimensions. Since this is public input via props and `SetAspectRatio`, an invalid fixed ratio can break the normalized geometry and render invalid CSS/ARIA values instead of being sanitized or ignored.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7o3B
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1017
parent_db_id: 3352802861
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Clamp direct crop inputs before storing**

`SetCropArea` is a public path for restored or externally edited crop geometry, but it stores the provided `CropArea` without normalizing finite coordinates or clamping `x + width` / `y + height` into the image. Passing an out-of-bounds crop immediately makes the API render invalid normalized CSS values and can later panic in resize/nudge paths that call `f64::clamp` with bounds derived from that invalid geometry.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G7o3E
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1274
parent_db_id: 3352802864
**<sub><sub>![P3 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P3-lightgrey?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Add empty alt text to the decorative image**

The Image part is rendered as an `<img>` with a `src` and `aria-hidden`, but no `alt`. When adapters spread these attrs onto the actual image, the markup still has a missing required alt attribute for a decorative image; add `alt=""` here so validators/axe and assistive-tech fallbacks treat the hidden source image consistently as decorative.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Normalize a rotation in degrees into the `(-180, 180]`-ish slider range the\n/// rotation control advertises, so repeated `r`/`R` rotations wrap instead of\n/// running past the declared `aria-valuemin`/`aria-valuemax`.\nfn normalize_rotation(degrees: f64) -> f64 {\n    (degrees + 180.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Normalize a rotation in degrees into the `(-180, 180]`-ish slider range the\n/// rotation control advertises, so repeated `r`/`R` rotations wrap instead of\n/// running past the declared `aria-valuemin`/`aria-valuemax`.\n///\n/// A non-finite input collapses to `0.0` so a stray `NaN`/`inf` rotation can\n/// never reach the rendered `aria-valuenow`/CSS.\nfn normalize_rotation(degrees: f64) -> f64 {\n    if !degrees.is_finite() {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    (degrees + 180.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0\n}\n\n/// Clamp `zoom` into the `[min, max]` band without ever panicking.\n///\n/// `f64::clamp` panics when `min > max` or a bound is `NaN`; both are reachable\n/// through the public `min_zoom`/`max_zoom` props. The bounds are ordered first\n/// and `.max().min()` is used (which yields the value unchanged when a bound is\n/// `NaN`), so a misconfigured cropper degrades to a safe range instead of taking\n/// down the app.\nfn clamp_zoom(zoom: f64, min: f64, max: f64) -> f64 {\n    let lower = min.min(max);\n    let upper = min.max(max);\n    zoom.max(lower).min(upper)\n}\n\n/// Coerce an externally-supplied crop area into a renderable, in-bounds value.\n///\n/// Non-finite fields fall back to the default crop; width/height are floored at\n/// [`MIN_CROP_SIZE`] and capped at the image; the origin is then pulled inside\n/// so `x + width <= 1` and `y + height <= 1`; rotation is normalized. This keeps\n/// `SetCropArea`/init/`SyncCrop` from storing geometry that would render invalid\n/// CSS or later invert a resize clamp and panic.\nfn sanitize_crop(area: CropArea) -> CropArea {\n    let default = CropArea::default();\n    let finite = |value: f64, fallback: f64| if value.is_finite() { value } else { fallback };\n\n    let width = finite(area.width, default.width).clamp(MIN_CROP_SIZE, 1.0);\n    let height = finite(area.height, default.height).clamp(MIN_CROP_SIZE, 1.0);\n    let x = finite(area.x, default.x).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - width);\n    let y = finite(area.y, default.y).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - height);\n\n    CropArea {\n        x,\n        y,\n        width,\n        height,\n        rotation: normalize_rotation(area.rotation),\n    }\n}"
}

  "new_string": "    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {\n        // Sanitize and ratio-constrain the initial crop *before* building the\n        // `Bindable`, so the constraint is reflected in the controlled slot that\n        // `get()` returns (not just the internal value), and a malformed\n        // `default_crop`/controlled `crop` can never seed out-of-bounds geometry.\n        let mut initial_crop = sanitize_crop(props.crop.unwrap_or(props.default_crop));\n        if let Some(ratio) = props.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n            constrain_to_ratio(&mut initial_crop, ratio);\n        }\n\n        let crop = if props.crop.is_some() {\n            Bindable::controlled(initial_crop)\n        } else {\n            Bindable::uncontrolled(initial_crop)\n        };\n\n        let ctx = Context {\n            crop,\n            aspect_ratio: props.aspect_ratio,\n            // Clamp the initial zoom so the first render never advertises a\n            // value outside `min_zoom..=max_zoom` (matches `SetZoom`/`SyncProps`).\n            zoom: clamp_zoom(props.zoom, props.min_zoom, props.max_zoom),\n            min_zoom: props.min_zoom,\n            max_zoom: props.max_zoom,\n            disabled: props.disabled,\n            circular: props.circular,\n            flip: props.flip,\n            drag_origin: None,\n            drag_start_crop: None,\n            focused_part: None,\n            locale: env.locale.clone(),\n            messages: messages.clone(),\n            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n        };\n\n        (State::Idle, ctx)\n    }"
}

925	    }
926	
927	    fn transition(
928	        state: &Self::State,
929	        event: &Self::Event,
930	        ctx: &Self::Context,
931	        props: &Self::Props,
932	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
933	        // Focus/Blur and the prop-sync events always pass through, regardless of
934	        // the disabled gate (prop sync must be able to re-enable the cropper).
935	        match event {
936	            Event::Focus { part } => {
937	                let part = *part;
938	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
939	                    ctx.focused_part = Some(part);
940	                }));
941	            }
942	
943	            Event::Blur { .. } => {
944	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {
945	                    ctx.focused_part = None;
946	                }));
947	            }
948	
949	            Event::SyncCrop => {
950	                let new_crop = props.crop;
951	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
952	                    if let Some(crop) = new_crop {
953	                        ctx.crop.set(crop);
954	                    }
955	
956	                    ctx.crop.sync_controlled(new_crop);
957	                }));
958	            }
959	
960	            Event::SyncProps => {
961	                let aspect_ratio = props.aspect_ratio;
962	                let zoom = props.zoom.clamp(props.min_zoom, props.max_zoom);
963	                let min_zoom = props.min_zoom;
964	                let max_zoom = props.max_zoom;
965	                let disabled = props.disabled;
966	                let circular = props.circular;
967	                let flip = props.flip;
968	
969	                // Becoming disabled mid-interaction must not strand the machine
970	                // in `Dragging`/`Resizing` — the disabled gate below would then
971	                // reject the trailing `DragEnd`/`ResizeEnd`. Cancel and return to
972	                // `Idle`.
973	                let cancel = disabled && !matches!(state, State::Idle);
974	
975	                let target = if cancel { State::Idle } else { *state };
976	
977	                return Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
978	                    ctx.aspect_ratio = aspect_ratio;
979	                    ctx.zoom = zoom;
980	                    ctx.min_zoom = min_zoom;
981	                    ctx.max_zoom = max_zoom;
982	                    ctx.disabled = disabled;
983	                    ctx.circular = circular;
984	                    ctx.flip = flip;
985	
986	                    if cancel {
987	                        ctx.drag_origin = None;
988	                        ctx.drag_start_crop = None;
989	                    }
990	
991	                    enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx);
992	                }));
993	            }

Cancel and return to\n                // `Idle`.\n                let cancel = disabled && !matches!(state, State::Idle);\n\n                let target = if cancel { State::Idle } else { *state };\n\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.aspect_ratio = aspect_ratio;\n                    ctx.zoom = zoom;\n                    ctx.min_zoom = min_zoom;\n                    ctx.max_zoom = max_zoom;\n                    ctx.disabled = disabled;\n                    ctx.circular = circular;\n                    ctx.flip = flip;\n\n                    if cancel {\n                        ctx.drag_origin = None;\n                        ctx.drag_start_crop = None;\n                    }\n\n                    enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx);\n                }));\n            }",
  "new_string": "            Event::SyncCrop => {\n                // Sanitize and ratio-constrain the parent's value, then store\n                // the constrained crop in *both* the internal and controlled\n                // slots so the rendered geometry (read via `get()`) satisfies the\n                // active aspect ratio — consistent with init/SetCropArea.\n                let aspect_ratio = props.aspect_ratio;\n                let new_crop = props.crop.map(|crop| {\n                    let mut crop = sanitize_crop(crop);\n                    if let Some(ratio) = aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n                        constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n                    }\n                    crop\n                });\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    if let Some(crop) = new_crop {\n                        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n                    }\n\n                    ctx.crop.sync_controlled(new_crop);\n                }));\n            }\n\n            Event::SyncProps => {\n                let aspect_ratio = props.aspect_ratio;\n                let min_zoom = props.min_zoom;\n                let max_zoom = props.max_zoom;\n                let disabled = props.disabled;\n                let circular = props.circular;\n\n                // Becoming disabled mid-interaction must not strand the machine\n                // in `Dragging`/`Resizing` — the disabled gate below would then\n                // reject the trailing `DragEnd`/`ResizeEnd`. Cancel and return to\n                // `Idle`.\n                let cancel = disabled && !matches!(state, State::Idle);\n\n                let target = if cancel { State::Idle } else { *state };\n\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.aspect_ratio = aspect_ratio;\n                    ctx.min_zoom = min_zoom;\n                    ctx.max_zoom = max_zoom;\n                    ctx.disabled = disabled;\n                    ctx.circular = circular;\n                    // `zoom`/`flip` are user-editable initial values, not synced\n                    // here; only re-clamp the *current* zoom into the (possibly\n                    // new) bounds so it never sits outside the slider range.\n                    ctx.zoom = clamp_zoom(ctx.zoom, min_zoom, max_zoom);\n\n                    if cancel {\n                        ctx.drag_origin = None;\n                        ctx.drag_start_crop = None;\n                    }\n\n                    enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx);\n                }));\n            }"
}

  "old_string": "    #[test]\n    fn sync_props_mirrors_config_and_cancels_active_interaction() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::DragStart { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Dragging);\n\n        // Disable mid-drag via props.\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().disabled(true).zoom(2.0)));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.context().disabled);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().zoom, 2.0);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().drag_origin, None);\n    }",
  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn sync_props_mirrors_config_and_cancels_active_interaction() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().circular(false));\n\n        // Establish a user-edited zoom so we can confirm it survives the sync.\n        drop(service.send(Event::SetZoom(2.5)));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DragStart { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Dragging);\n\n        // Disable + flip `circular` via props mid-drag.\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().disabled(true).circular(true)));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.context().disabled);\n        assert!(service.context().circular);\n        // `zoom` is an initial-only prop: the user's edit is preserved, not reset.\n        assert_eq!(service.context().zoom, 2.5);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().drag_origin, None);\n    }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "impl AspectRatio {\n    /// Get the ratio as a float.\n    pub fn as_ratio(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        match self {\n            Self::Free => None,\n            Self::Fixed(r) => Some(*r),\n            Self::Square => Some(1.0),\n            Self::Landscape4x3 => Some(4.0 / 3.0),\n            Self::Portrait3x4 => Some(3.0 / 4.0),\n            Self::Wide16x9 => Some(16.0 / 9.0),\n        }\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "impl AspectRatio {\n    /// Get the ratio as a float, or `None` when the crop is unconstrained.\n    ///\n    /// A `Fixed` ratio that is not finite and strictly positive (e.g. `0.0`, a\n    /// negative number, or `NaN`) is rejected as `None` — treated as\n    /// unconstrained — so it can never divide the crop geometry into infinite,\n    /// negative, or `NaN` dimensions.\n    pub fn as_ratio(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        match self {\n            Self::Fixed(r) if r.is_finite() && *r > 0.0 => Some(*r),\n            Self::Free | Self::Fixed(_) => None,\n            Self::Square => Some(1.0),\n            Self::Landscape4x3 => Some(4.0 / 3.0),\n            Self::Portrait3x4 => Some(3.0 / 4.0),\n            Self::Wide16x9 => Some(16.0 / 9.0),\n        }\n    }\n}"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01SBHX7sReiTpGf2TBpkchP9
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/spec/components/specialized/image-cropper.md",
  "old_string": "/// Normalize a rotation in degrees into the `[-180, 180]` range the rotation\n/// slider advertises, so repeated `r`/`R` rotations wrap instead of running\n/// past the declared `aria-valuemin`/`aria-valuemax`.\nfn normalize_rotation(degrees: f64) -> f64 {\n    (degrees + 180.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Normalize a rotation in degrees into the `[-180, 180]` range the rotation\n/// slider advertises, so repeated `r`/`R` rotations wrap instead of running\n/// past the declared `aria-valuemin`/`aria-valuemax`. A non-finite input\n/// collapses to `0.0` so a stray `NaN`/`inf` can never reach the rendered\n/// `aria-valuenow`/CSS.\nfn normalize_rotation(degrees: f64) -> f64 {\n    if !degrees.is_finite() {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    (degrees + 180.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0\n}\n\n/// Clamp `zoom` into `[min, max]` without ever panicking. `f64::clamp` panics\n/// when `min > max` or a bound is `NaN` — both reachable through the public\n/// `min_zoom`/`max_zoom` props — so the bounds are ordered first and `.max()`/\n/// `.min()` are used (yielding the value unchanged when a bound is `NaN`).\nconst fn clamp_zoom(zoom: f64, min: f64, max: f64) -> f64 {\n    let lower = min.min(max);\n    let upper = min.max(max);\n    zoom.max(lower).min(upper)\n}\n\n/// Coerce an externally-supplied crop area into a renderable, in-bounds value:\n/// non-finite fields fall back to the default crop, width/height are floored at\n/// `MIN_CROP_SIZE` and capped at the image, the origin is pulled inside so\n/// `x + width <= 1` and `y + height <= 1`, and rotation is normalized. Keeps\n/// `SetCropArea`/init/`SyncCrop` from storing geometry that renders invalid CSS\n/// or later inverts a resize clamp and panics.\nfn sanitize_crop(area: CropArea) -> CropArea {\n    let default = CropArea::default();\n    let finite = |value: f64, fallback: f64| if value.is_finite() { value } else { fallback };\n\n    let width = finite(area.width, default.width).clamp(MIN_CROP_SIZE, 1.0);\n    let height = finite(area.height, default.height).clamp(MIN_CROP_SIZE, 1.0);\n    let x = finite(area.x, default.x).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - width);\n    let y = finite(area.y, default.y).clamp(0.0, 1.0 - height);\n\n    CropArea { x, y, width, height, rotation: normalize_rotation(area.rotation) }\n}"
}
```

  "new_string": "    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {\n        // Sanitize and ratio-constrain the initial crop *before* building the\n        // `Bindable`, so the constraint is reflected in the controlled slot that\n        // `get()` returns (not just the internal value), and a malformed\n        // `default_crop`/controlled `crop` can never seed out-of-bounds geometry.\n        let mut initial_crop = sanitize_crop(props.crop.unwrap_or(props.default_crop));\n        if let Some(ratio) = props.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n            constrain_to_ratio(&mut initial_crop, ratio);\n        }\n\n        let crop = if props.crop.is_some() {\n            Bindable::controlled(initial_crop)\n        } else {\n            Bindable::uncontrolled(initial_crop)\n        };\n\n        let ids = ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id);\n        let locale = env.locale.clone();\n        let messages = messages.clone();\n\n        let ctx = Context {\n            crop,\n            aspect_ratio: props.aspect_ratio,\n            // Clamp the initial zoom so the first render never advertises a value\n            // outside `min_zoom..=max_zoom` (matches `SetZoom`/`SyncProps`).\n            zoom: clamp_zoom(props.zoom, props.min_zoom, props.max_zoom),\n            min_zoom: props.min_zoom,\n            max_zoom: props.max_zoom,\n            disabled: props.disabled,\n            circular: props.circular,\n            flip: props.flip,\n            drag_origin: None,\n            drag_start_crop: None,\n            focused_part: None,\n            locale,\n            messages,\n            ids,\n        };\n\n        (State::Idle, ctx)\n    }\n\n    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {\n        assert_eq!(old.id, new.id, \"image_cropper::Props.id must remain stable after init\");\n\n        let mut events = Vec::new();\n\n        if old.crop != new.crop {\n            events.push(Event::SyncCrop);\n        }\n\n        // `zoom` and `flip` are *initial* (uncontrolled) props that become\n        // user-editable state after mount — like `default_crop`. Changing them\n        // post-mount is intentionally ignored so a re-render that only touches an\n        // unrelated prop never clobbers the user's current zoom/flip.\n        if old.aspect_ratio != new.aspect_ratio\n            || old.min_zoom != new.min_zoom\n            || old.max_zoom != new.max_zoom\n            || old.disabled != new.disabled\n            || old.circular != new.circular\n        {\n            events.push(Event::SyncProps);\n        }\n\n        events\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "            Event::SyncCrop => {\n                // Sanitize and ratio-constrain the parent's value, then store the\n                // constrained crop in *both* the internal and controlled slots so\n                // the rendered geometry (read via `get()`) satisfies the active\n                // aspect ratio — consistent with init/SetCropArea.\n                let aspect_ratio = props.aspect_ratio;\n                let new_crop = props.crop.map(|crop| {\n                    let mut crop = sanitize_crop(crop);\n                    if let Some(ratio) = aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n                        constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n                    }\n                    crop\n                });\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    if let Some(crop) = new_crop {\n                        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n                    }\n                    ctx.crop.sync_controlled(new_crop);\n                }));\n            }\n            Event::SyncProps => {\n                let aspect_ratio = props.aspect_ratio;\n                let min_zoom = props.min_zoom;\n                let max_zoom = props.max_zoom;\n                let disabled = props.disabled;\n                let circular = props.circular;\n\n                // Becoming disabled mid-interaction must not strand the machine\n                // in `Dragging`/`Resizing`; cancel and return to `Idle`.\n                let cancel = disabled && !matches!(state, State::Idle);\n                let target = if cancel { State::Idle } else { *state };\n\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.aspect_ratio = aspect_ratio;\n                    ctx.min_zoom = min_zoom;\n                    ctx.max_zoom = max_zoom;\n                    ctx.disabled = disabled;\n                    ctx.circular = circular;\n                    // `zoom`/`flip` are user-editable initial values, not synced\n                    // here; only re-clamp the *current* zoom into the (possibly\n                    // new) bounds so it never sits outside the slider range.\n                    ctx.zoom = clamp_zoom(ctx.zoom, min_zoom, max_zoom);\n                    if cancel {\n                        ctx.drag_origin = None;\n                        ctx.drag_start_crop = None;\n                    }\n                    enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx);\n                }));\n            }"

I've addressed all 6 of Codex's second-pass findings (8 new failing-first tests, then fixes, spec synced, snapshots updated); `cargo xci-fast` is re-running. Summary while it runs:

| # | Codex finding | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `SyncCrop` doesn't enforce active ratio (controlled crops render unconstrained) | `SyncCrop` now sanitizes + `constrain_to_ratio`, storing the constrained value in **both** Bindable slots so `get()` reflects it. (My earlier "inert" reasoning was incomplete — the controlled *slot* must be constrained, not the internal.) Same fix applied at `init`. |
| 2 | `f64::clamp` panics on `min>max`/NaN bounds | New `clamp_zoom` orders bounds + uses `.max().min()` (NaN-tolerant); used in init/SyncProps/SetZoom/Reset |
| 3 | `SyncProps` clobbers user zoom/flip on unrelated prop change | `zoom`/`flip` are now treated as initial-only (uncontrolled): dropped from the `on_props_changed` trigger; `SyncProps` only re-clamps the *current* zoom into new bounds |
| 4 | `AspectRatio::Fixed` accepts 0/negative/NaN | `as_ratio` rejects non-finite/non-positive `Fixed` → `None` (unconstrained) |
| 5 | `SetCropArea` stores out-of-bounds geometry (invalid CSS + later resize panic) | New `sanitize_crop` (finite + floor + in-bounds + normalized rotation) applied at SetCropArea/init/SyncCrop/Reset |
| 6 | Decorative `<img>` missing `alt` | `image_attrs` sets `alt=""` |

The judgment calls (treating zoom/flip as initial-only à la `defaultValue`, rejecting invalid `Fixed` as unconstrained, constraining controlled values for consistency) I'll spell out in the inline replies. Once the gate is green I'll commit, reply+resolve the 6 threads, and re-trigger Codex.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G74xv
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1008
parent_db_id: 3352894503
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Sync controlled crop when aspect props change**

When `crop` is controlled and only `aspect_ratio` changes, `on_props_changed` emits `SyncProps` without `SyncCrop`, and this call only writes the constrained value to the bindable's internal `pending` slot. Because `Bindable::get()` still returns the old controlled crop, the rendered attrs/API can keep showing a non-square crop even after the parent switches `aspect_ratio` to `Square` (until the parent also changes `crop`). Mirror the constrained value into the controlled slot here, as `SyncCrop` does, or enqueue a crop sync when the ratio changes.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G74xx
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1491
parent_db_id: 3352894505
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Advertise ordered zoom slider bounds**

When callers accidentally pass inverted zoom bounds such as `min_zoom(3.0).max_zoom(1.0)`, initialization and `SetZoom` clamp against the ordered range, but the rendered slider still exposes `aria-valuemin="3.00"` and `aria-valuemax="1.00"`. That leaves the ARIA range invalid even though the internal value was hardened for this exact scenario; emit the same ordered bounds here that `clamp_zoom` uses.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G74xz
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1329
parent_db_id: 3352894508
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Expose disabled state to assistive tech**

For a disabled cropper, the root only gets the styling hook `data-ars-disabled`, while the workspace accessibility contract requires `aria-disabled="true"` for disabled composite widgets that remain focusable. Since the machine ignores interaction when disabled but the crop area/sliders can still be reached by keyboard, screen-reader users get no semantic indication that this `role="application"` widget is inoperable.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G74x2
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1026
parent_db_id: 3352894510
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Start controlled drags from the rendered crop**

In controlled mode, if the parent rejects or delays a previous `on_crop_change`, `pending()` can hold an unaccepted internal crop while `get()` is still the crop actually rendered from props. Starting the next drag from `pending()` makes subsequent drag/resize/nudge calculations jump from that stale internal geometry instead of the visible crop; use the current rendered controlled value as the interaction baseline and reserve `pending()` for reporting the just-computed callback value.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.
```

- **#2 (o74xx):** zoom slider advertises raw (possibly inverted) `aria-valuemin/max`. → order them like `clamp_zoom`.

- **#4 (o74x2):** interactions snapshot `pending()` (un-acked internal) instead of `get()` (rendered). → anchor drag/resize/nudge/rotation baselines to `get()`, reserve `pending()` for the callback report.

  "new_string": "/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.\n///\n/// The constrained value is written to the internal slot and, in controlled\n/// mode, mirrored into the controlled slot too — otherwise `get()` (and every\n/// rendered attr/API value) would keep returning the parent's unconstrained\n/// crop until the parent next changes `crop`, even though the ratio just\n/// changed (e.g. via `SetAspectRatio` or a `SyncProps` aspect change).\nfn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n        let mut crop = *ctx.crop.pending();\n        constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n        if ctx.crop.is_controlled() {\n            ctx.crop.sync_controlled(Some(crop));\n        }\n    }\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "    pub fn zoom_slider_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ZoomSlider.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"slider\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow),\n                format!(\"{:.2}\", self.ctx.zoom),\n            )\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin),\n                format!(\"{:.2}\", self.ctx.min_zoom),\n            )\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax),\n                format!(\"{:.2}\", self.ctx.max_zoom),\n            )",
  "new_string": "    pub fn zoom_slider_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ZoomSlider.data_attrs();\n\n        // Advertise *ordered* bounds — the same ordering `clamp_zoom` applies to\n        // the value — so inverted `min_zoom`/`max_zoom` props never produce an\n        // invalid `aria-valuemin > aria-valuemax` slider range.\n        let lower = self.ctx.min_zoom.min(self.ctx.max_zoom);\n        let upper = self.ctx.min_zoom.max(self.ctx.max_zoom);\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"slider\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow),\n                format!(\"{:.2}\", self.ctx.zoom),\n            )\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin), format!(\"{lower:.2}\"))\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax), format!(\"{upper:.2}\"))"
}

{

  "old_string": "/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.\nfn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n        // Build on the *pending* (internal) crop: in controlled mode `get()`\n        // returns the stale parent value until it round-trips through\n        // `on_crop_change`.\n        let mut crop = *ctx.crop.pending();\n        constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Re-constrain the current crop area to match the current aspect ratio.\n///\n/// The constrained value is written to the internal slot and, in controlled\n/// mode, mirrored into the controlled slot too — otherwise `get()` (and every\n/// rendered attr/API value) would keep returning the parent's unconstrained\n/// crop until the parent next changes `crop`, even though the ratio just\n/// changed (e.g. via `SetAspectRatio` or a `SyncProps` aspect change).\nfn enforce_aspect_ratio(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    if let Some(ratio) = ctx.aspect_ratio.as_ratio() {\n        let mut crop = *ctx.crop.pending();\n        constrain_to_ratio(&mut crop, ratio);\n        ctx.crop.set(crop);\n        if ctx.crop.is_controlled() {\n            ctx.crop.sync_controlled(Some(crop));\n        }\n    }\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Round-3 fixes done, spec synced, `cargo xci-fast` re-running. Summary while it runs:

| # | Codex finding | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `SyncProps` aspect change leaves controlled crop unconstrained on screen | `enforce_aspect_ratio` now mirrors the constrained value into the controlled slot too (so `get()` reflects it immediately) |
| 2 | Zoom slider advertises raw (possibly inverted) `aria-valuemin/max` | `zoom_slider_attrs` emits ordered bounds (`min.min(max)`/`min.max(max)`), matching `clamp_zoom` |
| 3 | Disabled root lacks `aria-disabled` | `root_attrs` sets `aria-disabled="true"` when disabled (+ §3.1 table row) |
| 4 | Interactions baseline off `pending()` (un-acked internal) | drag/resize/nudge/rotation now anchor to `get()` (rendered crop); `pending()` reserved for the callback report — exactly Codex's recommended split |

These complete the controlled-mode model: edits read the rendered value, report via `on_crop_change(pending())`, and the parent's acceptance (or the aspect constraint) updates the controlled slot. 84 unit tests pass, clippy clean, spec validated. Once the gate is green I'll push, reply+resolve the 4 threads, and re-trigger Codex.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G8HsW
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:742
parent_db_id: 3352978882
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Preserve the minimum size for extreme aspect ratios**

When consumers pass an extreme but valid `AspectRatio::Fixed` (for example `Fixed(100.0)`), this width-driven constraint can set `crop.height = width / ratio` below `MIN_CROP_SIZE` (the default crop becomes about `0.009` tall). Because this path is used during init, `SetAspectRatio`, and resize, the crop area can collapse into a nearly non-interactable strip despite the component contract/doc comments saying the 5% minimum is enforced; either reject/relax impossible ratios or choose dimensions so both axes stay above the floor when possible.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G8Hsc
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1641
parent_db_id: 3352978891
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Ignore root shortcuts when command modifiers are held**

When focus is inside the cropper, this handler also fires for modified browser/application shortcuts because it only checks the produced character. For example `Ctrl++`/`Cmd++` (browser zoom) also dispatches `SetZoom`, and `Ctrl+R`/`Cmd+R` can rotate the cropper before the browser/app handles reload; the keyboard contract lists plain `+/-` and `r/R`, so gate these shortcuts to unmodified events except for Shift where needed for uppercase/`+`.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6G8Hse
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs:1055
parent_db_id: 3352978894
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Reject non-finite pointer deltas before storing geometry**

If an adapter normalizes a pointer event while the image/viewport is not measurable (for example a zero-sized rect producing `NaN`), these clamps store `NaN` into `crop.x`/`crop.y` because `f64::clamp` does not sanitize a `NaN` value. That then renders `NaN` CSS/custom properties and can make later resize paths panic once the corrupted crop is used as the gesture baseline, so pointer-derived coordinates should be ignored or sanitized before updating the crop.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.
```

> AGENT

Fourth pass: 3 more, all valid:
- **#1 (o8HsW):** extreme `Fixed` ratios (e.g. 100:1) still collapse a dimension below 5% — geometrically a 100:1 crop can't keep both axes ≥5% in a unit square. → reject infeasible ratios in `as_ratio` (outside `[MIN, 1/MIN]`) and reposition in `constrain_to_ratio` so feasible ratios keep the floor near edges.
- **#2 (o8Hsc):** root shortcuts fire with Ctrl/Cmd held (Ctrl+R reload, Cmd++ zoom). → gate on no ctrl/alt/meta.
- **#3 (o8Hse):** non-finite pointer deltas store `NaN` (clamp doesn't sanitize). → drop coordinate events with non-finite values.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-296/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/image_cropper/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Re-shape `crop` to the given width:height `ratio`, keeping both dimensions\n/// within `[MIN_CROP_SIZE, image edge]`.\n///\n/// The width is the driving dimension: it is held at or above the floor that\n/// keeps *both* sides `>= MIN_CROP_SIZE` (`max(MIN, MIN * ratio)`) and at or\n/// below the largest width the right and bottom edges allow, then the height is\n/// derived from it. `.max().min()` (rather than `f64::clamp`) is deliberate —\n/// when the crop sits so close to an edge that the lower and upper bounds cross,\n/// fitting inside the image wins over the minimum-size floor instead of\n/// panicking.\nfn constrain_to_ratio(crop: &mut CropArea, ratio: f64) {\n    let lower = MIN_CROP_SIZE.max(MIN_CROP_SIZE * ratio);\n    let upper = (1.0 - crop.x).min((1.0 - crop.y) * ratio);\n\n    let width = crop.width.max(lower).min(upper);\n    crop.width = width;\n    crop.height = width / ratio;\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Re-shape `crop` to the given width:height `ratio`, keeping both dimensions\n/// `>= MIN_CROP_SIZE` and inside the unit square.\n///\n/// The width is the driving dimension. `lower` is the smallest width that keeps\n/// *both* sides at the floor (`max(MIN, MIN * ratio)`); `room` is the largest\n/// width the current origin allows before the right/bottom edges. When the\n/// origin leaves enough room (`lower <= room`) the caller's width is preserved\n/// within `[lower, room]`. When it does not, the crop is shrunk to `lower` and\n/// the origin is pulled inward so the minimum-size rect still fits — rather than\n/// collapsing a dimension below the floor. `ratio` is always feasible here\n/// because [`AspectRatio::as_ratio`] rejects ratios outside `[MIN, 1/MIN]`, so\n/// `lower <= 1.0` and `lower / ratio <= 1.0` and the reposition always fits.\nfn constrain_to_ratio(crop: &mut CropArea, ratio: f64) {\n    let lower = MIN_CROP_SIZE.max(MIN_CROP_SIZE * ratio);\n    let room = (1.0 - crop.x).min((1.0 - crop.y) * ratio);\n\n    let width = if lower <= room {\n        crop.width.clamp(lower, room)\n    } else {\n        lower\n    };\n\n    crop.width = width;\n    crop.height = width / ratio;\n    // Pull the origin inside the image in case `width`/`height` grew past the\n    // room at the current position (the reposition path above).\n    crop.x = crop.x.min(1.0 - crop.width).max(0.0);\n    crop.y = crop.y.min(1.0 - crop.height).max(0.0);\n}"
}

  "new_string": "    pub fn on_root_keydown(&self, data: &KeyboardEventData) {\n        // A printable key produced mid-IME-composition belongs to the composed\n        // text, not to a cropper shortcut.\n        if data.is_composing {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Ctrl/Cmd/Alt-modified keys are browser/app shortcuts (Ctrl+R reload,\n        // Cmd++ zoom, …); the cropper only owns the unmodified `+`/`-`/`r`/`v`\n        // keys. Shift is allowed — it produces `+` and uppercase `R`.\n        if data.ctrl_key || data.alt_key || data.meta_key {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        let Some(character) = data.character else {\n            return;\n        };"
}

The width drives the result: it is\n/// held at or above the floor that keeps *both* sides `>= MIN_CROP_SIZE`\n/// (`max(MIN, MIN * ratio)`) and at or below what the right/bottom edges allow,\n/// then the height is derived. `.max().min()` (not `f64::clamp`) is deliberate:\n/// when the crop sits so close to an edge that the bounds cross, fitting the\n/// image wins over the minimum-size floor instead of panicking.\nfn constrain_to_ratio(crop: &mut CropArea, ratio: f64) {\n    let lower = MIN_CROP_SIZE.max(MIN_CROP_SIZE * ratio);\n    let upper = (1.0 - crop.x).min((1.0 - crop.y) * ratio);\n    let width = crop.width.max(lower).min(upper);\n    crop.width = width;\n    crop.height = width / ratio;\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Re-shape `crop` to the given width:height `ratio`, keeping both dimensions\n/// `>= MIN_CROP_SIZE` and inside the unit square. `lower` is the smallest width\n/// that keeps both sides at the floor (`max(MIN, MIN * ratio)`); `room` is the\n/// largest width the current origin allows. When there's room (`lower <= room`)\n/// the caller's width is preserved within `[lower, room]`; otherwise the crop\n/// shrinks to `lower` and the origin is pulled inward so the minimum-size rect\n/// still fits, rather than collapsing a dimension. `ratio` is always feasible\n/// here (`as_ratio` rejects ratios outside `[MIN, 1/MIN]`), so the reposition\n/// always fits.\nfn constrain_to_ratio(crop: &mut CropArea, ratio: f64) {\n    let lower = MIN_CROP_SIZE.max(MIN_CROP_SIZE * ratio);\n    let room = (1.0 - crop.x).min((1.0 - crop.y) * ratio);\n    let width = if lower <= room { crop.width.clamp(lower, room) } else { lower };\n    crop.width = width;\n    crop.height = width / ratio;\n    crop.x = crop.x.min(1.0 - crop.width).max(0.0);\n    crop.y = crop.y.min(1.0 - crop.height).max(0.0);\n}"
}

> AGENT

Round-4 fixes done (88 tests, clippy clean, spec synced); `cargo xci-fast` re-running. Summary:

| # | Codex finding | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Extreme `Fixed` ratio (100:1) collapses a dimension below 5% | `as_ratio` rejects ratios outside the feasible band `[0.05, 20]` (treated unconstrained); `constrain_to_ratio` repositions the origin so feasible ratios keep the floor near edges |
| 2 | Root shortcuts fire with Ctrl/Cmd held (Ctrl+R reload, Cmd++ zoom) | `on_root_keydown` ignores events with ctrl/alt/meta (Shift still allowed for `+`/`R`) |
| 3 | Non-finite pointer deltas store `NaN` (clamp doesn't sanitize) | `transition` drops `DragStart/Move`, `ResizeStart/Move`, `NudgeCrop` events with non-finite coordinates |

Once the gate is green I'll push, reply+resolve the 3 threads, and re-trigger Codex.
